Implement the following plan:

# Grounding Prompt Variant Experiment

## Context

We added 2 new grounding test snippets to `test_snippets.json` based on failures discovered in the latest protocol extraction:
1. "Primary indication for TKA is degenerative osteoarthritis of the knee." (entities lost during grounding)
2. Full compound "Female subjects must be surgically sterile; or at least 2 years postmenopausal;..." (decomposition failure)

We need to run ALL 11 grounding snippets (~24 entities) through 3 prompt variants to find which gets everything right: codes, relations, values, and units. Each variant's calls are traced to MLFlow so we can analyze failure patterns and decide whether a 4th variant is needed.

## The 3 Variants

| Variant | MedGemma (code selection) | Field Mapper (relation/value/unit) |
|---------|--------------------------|-------------------------------------|
| **A: Baseline** | Current production prompts | Current production prompts |
| **B: Targeted Fixes** | `exact_match_first` evaluate addition | `operator_rules_v2` field mapper rules |
| **C: Enhanced** | `exact_match_first` + boolean/categorical guidance in system prompt | `operator_rules_v2` + worked examples for boolean entities, score-type values, compound criterion negation |

- **Variant A** is the control (current code)
- **Variant B** combines the two best-performing fragments from the existing `prompt_variants.py` A/B tests
- **Variant C** extends B with new prompt additions targeting the failure modes in the new test cases

## Variant C Prompt Additions (New)

**System prompt addition** (appended to `grounding_system.jinja2`):
```
11. For boolean/presence entities (gender, conditions, procedures), when the
    entity text is a simple medical concept with no numeric qualifier, always
    select the broadest matching concept code. Do not let criterion complexity
    distract from the entity being grounded.
```

**Field mapper rules addition** (appended to `operator_rules_v2`):
```
- For boolean/presence entities appearing in compound criteria joined by OR/semicolons,
  map ONLY the specific entity to relation='=', value='True'. Ignore other branches.
  Example: criterion "surgically sterile; or postmenopausal; or practicing contraception"
  with entity "Postmenopause" → relation='=', value='True', unit=null
- For ordinal scoring systems (ASA, ECOG, NYHA, etc.) with discrete listed values like
  "1, 2, or 3", use relation='=' with the enumerated values as-is. Do NOT convert to a range.
```

## Script Architecture

**New file:** `tests/e2e/run_variant_experiment.py`

Builds on patterns from `run_prompt_variants.py` and `run_grounding_snippets.py`:

### Phase 1: Cache (one-time API calls)
- `TerminologyRouter.route_entity()` per entity → candidates cache
- `normalize_unit()` per entity → unit cache (deterministic, no LLM)

### Phase 2: Run 3 Variants
For each variant (A, B, C):
1. Tag an MLFlow experiment run with `variant={name}`, `experiment_type=prompt_variant`
2. For each entity:
   - `medgemma_decide()` with variant-specific prompts → `selected_code`, `confidence`
   - `generate_field_mappings()` with variant-specific prompts → `relation`, `value`, `unit`
3. Capture per-entity trace data: latency_ms, token counts (from MedGemma + Gemini), error/retry count

### Phase 3: Score & Compare
Score each variant on 5 dimensions per entity:
- **Code match**: `selected_code == expected`
- **Relation match**: `field_mapping.relation == expected`
- **Value match**: `field_mapping.value == expected`
- **Unit UCUM match**: from `normalize_unit()` cache
- **Unit OMOP match**: from `normalize_unit()` cache

### Phase 4: MLFlow Trace Analysis
After all 3 variants complete, query the MLFlow trace store to analyze:

1. **Failure pattern clustering** — group mismatches by entity type (Condition, Procedure, Demographic, Medication, Lab_Value) and failure dimension (code/relation/value). Identifies whether failures are entity-type-specific or prompt-generic.
2. **Confidence vs accuracy** — for each variant, plot confidence scores against actual correctness. Low confidence + correct = conservative model. High confidence + wrong = dangerous hallucination. This tells us if the model "knows what it doesn't know."
3. **Retry effectiveness** — count how many entities required retries per variant and whether retries improved or degraded the outcome. High retry count with no improvement = wasted API budget.
4. **Latency breakdown** — compare median/p95 latency per variant. If variant C is significantly slower without meaningful accuracy gain, B may be preferable.

Trace data is collected inline during Phase 2 (each `medgemma_decide` and `generate_field_mappings` call already writes MLFlow spans via the existing tracing infrastructure). The script reads back trace metrics from the SQLite store at `.mlflow/mlflow.db` after all variants complete.

### Phase 5: Decision — Stop or Continue?
The script prints a recommendation based on the results:

| Outcome | Action |
|---------|--------|
| One variant scores 100% on all dimensions | **Stop.** Declare winner. Print the prompt diff to apply to production. |
| Best variant has ≤2 failures and they're in one entity type | **Stop.** Flag the specific entity type for a targeted 4th variant. Print what the 4th variant should address. |
| Best variant has >2 failures spread across types | **Continue.** Auto-generate a Variant D hypothesis from the failure pattern analysis: which prompt section to change and what the change should be. Save the hypothesis to `prompt_variant_results/variant_d_hypothesis.md`. |
| All variants perform equally (no improvement over baseline) | **Stop.** The prompts aren't the bottleneck — investigate TerminologyRouter candidate quality or model capability instead. |

## Files to Create/Modify

| File | Action |
|------|--------|
| `tests/e2e/run_variant_experiment.py` | **Create** — Main experiment script (~350 lines) |
| `tests/e2e/prompt_variants.py` | **Edit** — Add Variant C definitions (ENHANCED_SYSTEM, ENHANCED_FIELD_MAPPER constants) |

No production code changes. This is a read-only experiment.

## API Budget

~24 router calls (cached) + 24×3 MedGemma/Gemini decide + 24×3 field mapper = ~24 + 144 + 72 = ~240 total calls. `asyncio.Semaphore(4)` for rate limiting. Expected runtime: 5-8 minutes.

## Execution

```bash
set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python tests/e2e/run_variant_experiment.py
```

## 1. Test Snippets

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_snippets.json`

This JSON file contains two test suites:

- **`extraction_test_snippets`** (7 items): Each has `snippet_text`, `extracted_criteria`, and `classification` ("inclusion"/"exclusion"/"neither"). Used for the extraction pipeline.

- **`grounding_test_snippets`** (11 items): Each has `snippet_text` and `entities[]`. Each entity has:
  - `entity_name` (string)
  - `system` ("UMLS" or "SNOMED")
  - `code` (CUI string like "C0201976")
  - `relation` (operator: ">", "<", "=", "!=")
  - `value` (string)
  - `unit` (human-readable, nullable)
  - `unit_ucum` (UCUM code, nullable)
  - `unit_omop_concept_id` (int, nullable)

Some snippets have a `_failure_context` field documenting known failure modes (e.g., truncation by `DEV_MAX_ENTITIES=5`, or Gemini decomposition failures).

---

## 2. Prompt Variant Definitions

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/prompt_variants.py`

A pure-data module with no side effects. Defines two variant lists:

### `MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS: list[dict]`
Each dict has keys: `name`, `grounding_system` (extra text appended to grounding_system.jinja2), `grounding_evaluate` (extra text appended to grounding_evaluate.jinja2).

Five variants:
1. **baseline** -- no modifications
2. **specificity_guard** -- adds rules 9-10 to system prompt (prefer parent concept, prefer primary CUI)
3. **context_emphasis** -- adds cross-check to evaluate prompt (reject over-specific candidates)
4. **combined** -- both system + evaluate modifications
5. **exact_match_first** -- adds mandatory exact-match-first protocol to evaluate prompt

### `FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS: list[dict]`
Each dict has keys: `name`, `field_mapper_rules_extra` (extra rules injected before `</rules>` in prompt).

Three variants:
1. **baseline** -- no modifications
2. **operator_rules** -- rules for verbatim operator extraction, "within" usage, negation patterns
3. **operator_rules_v2** -- stronger CRITICAL rule for literal operator reading with a worked BMI example

---

## 3. Prompt Variant Runner

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/run_prompt_variants.py`

### Key Imports:
```python
from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import _get_router
from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
from protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import generate_field_mappings
from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template as _original_render_template
from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import medgemma_decide
```

### Data Structures:
- `_CodeMismatchEntity(TypedDict)`: `snippet_idx`, `entity_idx`, `entity_name`, `expected_code`
- `_RelationMismatchEntity(TypedDict)`: `snippet_idx`, `entity_idx`, `entity_name`, `expected_relation`, `golden_code`, `golden_system`, `golden_preferred_term`
- `CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES` -- 6 entities with known code failures
- `RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES` -- 3 entities with known relation failures

### Patching Helpers:

**`_make_patched_render(variant: dict)`**: Returns a function that wraps `render_template`, appending variant-specific text to targeted template names (`grounding_system.jinja2` and `grounding_evaluate.jinja2`).

**`_PromptModifyingLLM`**: Wrapper around a LangChain RunnableSequence that intercepts `ainvoke`/`invoke` calls and injects extra rules before `</rules>` tags in prompts.

**`_make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules: str)`**: Returns a patched `create_structured_llm` that wraps the returned LLM in `_PromptModifyingLLM`.

### Test Functions:

**`async _run_code_mismatch_tests(snippets) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]`**
- Step 1: Cache `TerminologyRouter` candidates per entity (one API call each)
- Step 2: For each MedGemma variant, patch `render_template` and call `medgemma_decide`
- Returns `{variant_name: {entity_label: selected_code_or_None}}`

**`async _run_relation_mismatch_tests(snippets) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]`**
- Builds synthetic `EntityGroundingResult` from golden data
- For each field mapper variant, patches `create_structured_llm` and calls `generate_field_mappings`
- Returns `{variant_name: {entity_label: relation_or_None}}`

**`async main()`**: Runs both test sets, prints comparison tables, saves JSON to `tests/e2e/prompt_variant_results/latest.json`.

## 5. Core Pipeline Functions

### `medgemma_decide`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/medgemma_decider.py`

```python
async def medgemma_decide(
    entity: dict[str, Any],         # keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id
    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
    criterion_context: str,
) -> EntityGroundingResult:
```

Flow: Renders `grounding_system.jinja2` + `grounding_evaluate.jinja2`, sends to MedGemma, then structures the raw response with `_structure_decision_with_gemini()` which uses Gemini with `GroundingDecision` schema.

**`GroundingDecision` Pydantic model:** `selected_code`, `selected_system`, `preferred_term`, `confidence` (0.0-1.0), `reasoning`.

**`async agentic_reasoning_loop(entity, criterion_context, router, attempt=1) -> AgenticReasoningResult`**: Called on failed grounding. Uses `grounding_reasoning.jinja2`. Returns `should_skip`, `is_derived`, `derived_term`, `rephrased_query`, `gemini_suggestion`, `reasoning`.

### `generate_field_mappings`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

```python
async def generate_field_mappings(
    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
    criterion_text: str,
) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
```

Uses `create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)` and `field_mapping.jinja2`. Each returned mapping dict has keys: `entity`, `relation`, `value` (dict with `type`/`value`/`unit`), `entity_code`, `entity_system`, `omop_concept_id`, `entity_type`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system`.

**`RelationOperator`**: Literal type: `"=", "!=", ">", ">=", "<", "<=", "within", "not_in_last", "contains", "not_contains"`.

**`FieldMappingItem` Pydantic model:** `entity`, `relation` (validated/normalized), `value` (FieldMappingValue), `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system`.

### `normalize_unit`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/unit_normalizer.py`

```python
def normalize_unit(unit_text: str | None) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]:
```

Pure DB lookup (OMOP UCUM vocabulary), cached with `lru_cache(512)`. Returns `(ucum_code, omop_unit_concept_id)`.

Also provides:
- `normalize_value(value_text) -> tuple[str | None, int | None]` -- categorical value normalization
- `normalize_ordinal_value(value_text, entity_text) -> tuple[int | None, int | None] | None` -- ordinal scale normalization (ECOG, NYHA, ASA, Karnofsky)

### `TerminologyRouter`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/terminology_router.py`

```python
class TerminologyRouter:
    def __init__(self, config_path: Path | None = None) -> None:
    def get_apis_for_entity(self, entity_type: str) -> list[str]:
    async def route_entity(self, entity_text: str, entity_type: str) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
    async def _query_tooluniverse(self, api_name: str, entity_text: str) -> list[GroundingCandidate]:
```

Config loaded from `config/routing.yaml`. Entity types map to API lists. Retry with tenacity (3 attempts, exponential backoff). `PermanentAPIError` and `TransientAPIError` exceptions. Smart acronym routing adds UMLS for short all-caps terms. Diskcache TTL = 7 days.

## 6. Grounding Schemas

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/grounding.py`

```python
class GroundingCandidate(BaseModel):
    source_api: str
    code: str
    preferred_term: str
    semantic_type: str | None
    score: float  # 0.0-1.0

class EntityGroundingResult(BaseModel):
    entity_text: str
    entity_type: str
    selected_code: str | None
    selected_system: str | None
    preferred_term: str | None
    confidence: float  # 0.0-1.0
    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate]
    reasoning: str
    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]] | None
    criterion_id: str | None
    omop_concept_id: str | None
    reconciliation_status: str | None

class GroundingBatchResult(BaseModel):
    results: list[EntityGroundingResult]
    errors: list[str]
```

## 7. Ground Node (Orchestrator)

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/nodes/ground.py`

Key functions:
- **`_get_router() -> TerminologyRouter`**: Singleton factory.
- **`_reconcile_dual_grounding(result, omop_result)`**: Fuzzy-matches ToolUniverse vs OMOP results. Sets `omop_concept_id` and `reconciliation_status`.
- **`async _ground_entity_with_retry(entity, router, criterion_text) -> tuple[EntityGroundingResult, int]`**: 3-attempt agentic retry loop.
- **`async _ground_entity_parallel(entity, router, criterion_text, entity_num, total, semaphore)`**: Concurrency-controlled wrapper. Runs dual grounding (TerminologyRouter + OMOP) in parallel, generates field mappings, grounds categorical values.
- **`async ground_node(state: PipelineState) -> dict`**: Main pipeline node. Processes entities in parallel with `asyncio.gather`, handles consent skipping, `DEV_MAX_ENTITIES` truncation, MedGemma warmup, audit logging.

## 8. Jinja2 Templates

### `grounding_system.jinja2`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/grounding_system.jinja2`

System prompt for MedGemma as a medical terminology expert. Contains confidence scoring rubric (0.9-1.0 exact, 0.7-0.8 synonym, 0.5-0.6 partial, 0.0 no match) and 10 rules including specificity guard and primary CUI preference (rules 9-10 are already baked in from a previous variant promotion).

### `grounding_evaluate.jinja2`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/grounding_evaluate.jinja2`

Template variables: `entity_text`, `entity_type`, `criterion_context`, `candidates`. Lists candidates with source/code/term/semantic/score. Instructs model to select the best match.

### `field_mapping.jinja2`
**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/field_mapping.jinja2`

Template variables: `grounded_term`, `code_context`, `criterion_text`. Contains `<rules>` section with operator extraction rules, boolean normalization, value types, temporal durations, relative value resolution, and negation patterns. Has `</rules>` closing tag (important for variant injection pattern).

### `grounding_reasoning.jinja2`

Template variables: `entity_text`, `entity_type`, `criterion_context`, `previous_query`, `attempt`. Three reasoning questions: Q1 (valid?), Q2 (derived?), Q3 (rephrase?).

### `structure_decision.jinja2`

## 9. MLflow Tracing Infrastructure

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tracing.py`

### Context Variables:
- `_pipeline_run_id: ContextVar[str]` -- set per pipeline invocation
- `_pipeline_experiment_id: ContextVar[str | None]` -- set after MLflow setup

### Key Functions:

```python
@contextmanager
def pipeline_span(name: str, span_type: str = "CHAIN", protocol_id: str = "") -> Generator[Any, None, None]:
```
Creates a separate MLflow top-level trace per node. Tags with `node`, `protocol_id`, `run_id`. Falls back to `_NoOpSpan` when MLflow is unavailable.

```python
@contextmanager
def llm_span(name: str, model_name: str = "") -> Generator[Any, None, None]:
```
Creates a child span for individual LLM calls. Returns `_LLMSpanCtx` with `set_request(prompt_text)` and `set_response(response_text, usage?)`. Falls back to `_NoOpLLMSpan`.

### Classes:
- **`_NoOpSpan`**: `set_inputs()`, `set_outputs()`, `set_status()` -- all no-ops
- **`_LLMSpanCtx`**: `set_request(prompt)`, `set_response(response, usage?)` -- wraps MLflow span
- **`_NoOpLLMSpan`**: No-op replacement for `_LLMSpanCtx`

Tests 3 properties: real-time trace appearance, independent traces, and grouping tags. Uses a mini LangGraph with 3 nodes.

## Key Patterns for Implementing a Grounding Prompt Variant Experiment

1. **Variant injection for MedGemma**: Patch `render_template` to append text to `grounding_system.jinja2` and/or `grounding_evaluate.jinja2` output.

2. **Variant injection for field mapper**: Patch `create_structured_llm` to wrap the LLM with `_PromptModifyingLLM` that injects rules before `</rules>`.

3. **Test data**: Use `grounding_test_snippets` from `test_snippets.json` with golden `code`, `relation`, `value`, `unit`, `unit_ucum`, `unit_omop_concept_id`.

4. **API call flow**: `TerminologyRouter.route_entity()` -> cache candidates -> `medgemma_decide()` per variant (for code tests) or `generate_field_mappings()` per variant (for relation tests).

5. **MLflow tracing**: Uses `pipeline_span` (top-level) and `llm_span` (child) context managers. All LLM calls are traced with request/response.

6. **sys.path setup pattern**: Insert `services/protocol-processor-service/src`, `services/api-service/src`, and `libs/shared/src` into `sys.path`.

```
     1→"""Prompt variant definitions for A/B testing grounding failures.
     2→
     3→Pure data module — no side effects, no imports beyond stdlib.
     4→
     5→Each MedGemma variant has:
     6→- name: short label
     7→- grounding_system: extra text to append to grounding_system.jinja2 output (or None)
     8→- grounding_evaluate: extra text to append to grounding_evaluate.jinja2 output (or None)
     9→
    10→Each field mapper variant has:
    11→- name: short label
    12→- field_mapper_rules_extra: extra rules to inject before </rules> in prompt (or None)
    13→"""
    14→
    15→# ── MedGemma variant fragments ──────────────────────────────────────────────
    16→
    17→_SPECIFICITY_GUARD_SYSTEM = (
    18→    "\n9. When the entity text contains no qualifier (e.g., no age, stage, "
    19→    "site, or laterality modifier), prefer the PARENT concept over "
    20→    "subspecific variants. For example, 'Ankylosing Spondylitis' maps to "
    21→    "the general concept (C0038013), not 'Juvenile Ankylosing Spondylitis'."
    22→    "\n10. When multiple candidates share near-identical preferred terms "
    23→    "(differing only by version, alternate CUI, or source), prefer the "
    24→    "PRIMARY CUI — the one whose preferred term is the most concise and "
    25→    "canonical match for the entity text."
    26→)
    27→
    28→_CONTEXT_EMPHASIS_EVALUATE = (
    29→    "\n\n## Cross-check\n"
    30→    "Before making your final selection, verify:\n"
    31→    "- If the candidate's preferred term adds a qualifier NOT present in "
    32→    "the entity text (e.g., 'Juvenile', 'Ectopic', 'Chronic', 'Bilateral'), "
    33→    "it is OVER-SPECIFIC — prefer the unqualified parent concept.\n"
    34→    "- If two candidates have near-identical preferred terms, prefer the "
    35→    "one whose CUI is the primary/canonical entry, not an alternate or "
    36→    "version-specific code."
    37→)
    38→
    39→# Diagnosis from MLflow traces: the `combined` variant fails because stacking
    40→# rules in BOTH system + evaluate overloads the model — it acknowledges the
    41→# rules then ignores them. The `exact_match_first` variant puts ONE concise
    42→# procedural instruction in the evaluate template: scan for exact matches
    43→# first, only fall through to subspecific candidates if none found.
    44→_EXACT_MATCH_FIRST_EVALUATE = (
    45→    "\n\n## MANDATORY — Exact-Match-First Protocol\n"
    46→    "BEFORE analyzing candidates, follow this procedure:\n"
    47→    "1. Scan the candidate list for any entry whose preferred_term matches "
    48→    "the entity text exactly (case-insensitive, ignoring minor word order).\n"
    49→    "2. If one or more exact matches exist, select the one with the highest "
    50→    "score. Do NOT consider subspecific variants that add qualifiers "
    51→    "(e.g., 'Juvenile', 'Chronic', region-specific) when an exact match "
    52→    "is available.\n"
    53→    "3. Only if NO exact match exists, proceed to analyze close synonyms."
    54→)
    55→
    56→# ── Field mapper variant fragments ──────────────────────────────────────────
    57→
    58→_OPERATOR_RULES_EXTRA = (
    59→    "\n- Extract comparison operators VERBATIM from the criterion text. "
    60→    'Never invert the operator direction. If the text says ">44", '
    61→    'the relation is ">", not "<".'
    62→    '\n- Use "within" ONLY for numeric min/max ranges (e.g., "between '
    63→    '18 and 65"). Do NOT use "within" for discrete categorical lists '
    64→    'like "1, 2, or 3" — use "=" instead.'
    65→    '\n- Negation patterns: "non-", "not ", "no " preceding an entity '
    66→    "indicate ABSENCE. Map to relation='!=' with value='True'. "
    67→    "For example, \"non-pregnant\" means Pregnancy relation='!=' value='True'."
    68→)
    69→
    70→# v2: adds an explicit worked example for the BMI inversion failure.
    71→# Trace analysis showed the LLM reads "body mass index >44" and generates
    72→# relation='<' — interpreting it as an upper-bound exclusion rather than
    73→# reading the operator literally. A concrete example forces the right parse.
    74→_OPERATOR_RULES_V2_EXTRA = (
    75→    "\n- CRITICAL: Read the mathematical operator from the criterion text "
    76→    "LITERALLY. The relation MUST match the symbol in the text. "
    77→    "Example: 'body mass index >44 kg/m2' → relation='>', value='44'. "
    78→    "Do NOT interpret clinical intent (e.g., upper-limit exclusion). "
    79→    "Just read the symbol."
    80→    '\n- Use "within" ONLY for numeric min/max ranges (e.g., "between '
    81→    '18 and 65"). Do NOT use "within" for discrete categorical lists '
    82→    'like "1, 2, or 3" — use "=" instead.'
    83→    '\n- Negation patterns: "non-", "not ", "no " preceding an entity '
    84→    "indicate ABSENCE. Map to relation='!=' with value='True'. "
    85→    "For example, \"non-pregnant\" means Pregnancy relation='!=' value='True'."
    86→)
    87→
    88→# ── Variant lists ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
    89→
    90→MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS: list[dict] = [

    94→        "grounding_evaluate": None,
```

```
     2→"""A/B testing harness for prompt variants on grounding failures.

   141→# ── Patching helpers ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   142→
   143→
   144→def _make_patched_render(variant: dict):
   145→    """Create a patched _render_template for the given MedGemma variant.
   146→
   147→    The wrapper calls the original _render_template, then appends variant-
   148→    specific text to the rendered output for targeted template names.
   149→    """
   150→
   151→    def patched(template_name: str, **kwargs) -> str:
   152→        rendered = _original_render_template(template_name, **kwargs)
   153→        if template_name == "grounding_system.jinja2" and variant.get(
   154→            "grounding_system"
   155→        ):
   156→            rendered += variant["grounding_system"]
   157→        if template_name == "grounding_evaluate.jinja2" and variant.get(
   158→            "grounding_evaluate"
   159→        ):
   160→            rendered += variant["grounding_evaluate"]
   161→        return rendered
   162→
   163→    return patched
   164→
   165→
   166→class _PromptModifyingLLM:
   167→    """Wrapper around a LangChain RunnableSequence that modifies prompts.
   168→
   169→    LangChain's .with_structured_output() returns a RunnableSequence (Pydantic
   170→    model) that doesn't allow attribute assignment. This wrapper delegates all
   171→    calls to the real LLM but injects extra rules into the prompt.
   172→    """
   173→
   174→    def __init__(self, real_llm, extra_rules: str):
   175→        self._real = real_llm
   176→        self._extra = extra_rules
   177→
   178→    async def ainvoke(self, prompt, *args, **kwargs):
   179→        modified = prompt.replace("</rules>", self._extra + "\n</rules>")
   180→        return await self._real.ainvoke(modified, *args, **kwargs)
   181→
   182→    def invoke(self, prompt, *args, **kwargs):
   183→        modified = prompt.replace("</rules>", self._extra + "\n</rules>")
   184→        return self._real.invoke(modified, *args, **kwargs)
   185→
   186→    def __getattr__(self, name):
   187→        return getattr(self._real, name)
   188→
   189→
   190→def _make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules: str):
   191→    """Create a patched create_structured_llm that injects extra rules.
   192→
   193→    Returns a drop-in replacement for create_structured_llm. The returned
   194→    wrapper modifies prompts to insert extra rules before </rules>.
   195→    """
   196→    from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
   197→        create_structured_llm as _real_create,
   198→    )
   199→
   200→    def patched_create(output_schema):
   201→        real_llm = _real_create(output_schema)
   202→        if real_llm is None:
   203→            return None
   204→        return _PromptModifyingLLM(real_llm, extra_rules)
   205→
   206→    return patched_create
   207→
   208→
   209→# ── Code mismatch testing ──────────────────────────────────────────────────
   210→
   211→
   212→async def _run_code_mismatch_tests(
   213→    snippets: list[dict[str, str]],
   214→) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]:
   215→    """Run code mismatch entities through each MedGemma variant.
   216→
   217→    Step 1: Cache TerminologyRouter candidates per entity (one API call each).
   218→    Step 2: For each variant, patch _render_template and call medgemma_decide.
   219→
   220→    Returns: {variant_name: {entity_label: selected_code_or_None}}
   221→    """
   222→    router = _get_router()
   223→    results: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {}
   224→
   225→    # Step 1: Cache candidates
   226→    print("\n  Caching TerminologyRouter candidates...")
   227→    cached_candidates: dict[str, list] = {}
   228→    for ent in CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   229→        label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   230→        entity_name = ent["entity_name"]
   231→        candidates = await router.route_entity(entity_name, "Condition")
   232→        cached_candidates[label] = candidates
   233→        print(f"    {label} '{entity_name}': {len(candidates)} candidates")
   234→
   235→    # Step 2: Run each variant
   236→    for variant in MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS:
   237→        vname = variant["name"]
   238→        print(f"\n  Variant: {vname}")
   239→        results[vname] = {}
   240→
   241→        for ent in CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   242→            label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   243→            entity_name = ent["entity_name"]
   244→            snippet = snippets[ent["snippet_idx"]]
   245→            criterion_text = snippet["snippet_text"]
   246→            candidates = cached_candidates[label]
   247→
   248→            entity_dict = {
   249→                "text": entity_name,
   250→                "entity_type": "Condition",
   251→                "criterion_text": criterion_text,
   252→            }
   253→
   254→            patched_render = _make_patched_render(variant)
   255→            with patch(
   256→                "protocol_processor.prompts.render_template",
   257→                new=patched_render,
   258→            ):
   259→                try:
   260→                    result = await medgemma_decide(
   261→                        entity_dict, candidates, criterion_text
   262→                    )
   263→                    code = result.selected_code
   264→                except Exception as e:
   265→                    print(f"    {label} '{entity_name}': ERROR — {e}")
   266→                    code = None
   267→
   268→            match = "OK" if code == ent["expected_code"] else "MISS"
   269→            print(f"    {label} '{entity_name}': {code} [{match}]")
   270→            results[vname][label] = code
   271→
   272→    return results
   273→
   274→
   275→# ── Relation mismatch testing ──────────────────────────────────────────────
   276→
   277→
   278→async def _run_relation_mismatch_tests(
   279→    snippets: list[dict[str, str]],
   280→) -> dict[str, dict[str, str | None]]:
   281→    """Run relation mismatch entities through each field mapper variant.
   282→
   283→    Uses synthetic EntityGroundingResult objects built from golden data
   284→    (no TerminologyRouter or MedGemma calls needed).
   285→
   286→    Returns: {variant_name: {entity_label: relation_or_None}}
   287→    """
   288→    results: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]] = {}
   289→
   290→    for variant in FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS:
   291→        vname = variant["name"]
   292→        print(f"\n  Variant: {vname}")
   293→        results[vname] = {}
   294→
   295→        for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   296→            label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   297→            snippet = snippets[ent["snippet_idx"]]
   298→            criterion_text = snippet["snippet_text"]
   299→
   300→            # Build synthetic EntityGroundingResult from golden data
   301→            entity_result = EntityGroundingResult(
   302→                entity_text=ent["entity_name"],
   303→                entity_type="Condition",
   304→                selected_code=ent["golden_code"],
   305→                selected_system=ent["golden_system"],
   306→                preferred_term=ent["golden_preferred_term"],
   307→                confidence=0.9,
   308→                candidates=[],
   309→                reasoning="A/B test harness — golden code used",
   310→            )
   311→
   312→            extra_rules = variant.get("field_mapper_rules_extra")
   313→            try:
   314→                if extra_rules:
   315→                    wrapper = _make_field_mapper_llm_wrapper(extra_rules)
   316→                    with patch(
   317→                        "protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper.create_structured_llm",
   318→                        new=wrapper,
   319→                    ):
   320→                        mappings = await generate_field_mappings(
   321→                            entity_result, criterion_text
   322→                        )
   323→                else:
   324→                    mappings = await generate_field_mappings(
   325→                        entity_result, criterion_text
   326→                    )
   327→                relation = mappings[0].get("relation") if mappings else None
   328→            except Exception as e:
   329→                print(f"    {label} '{ent['entity_name']}': ERROR — {e}")
   330→                relation = None
   331→
   332→            match = "OK" if relation == ent["expected_relation"] else "MISS"
   333→            print(f"    {label} '{ent['entity_name']}': {relation} [{match}]")
   334→            results[vname][label] = relation
   335→
   336→    return results
   337→
   338→
   339→# ── Report printing ─────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   340→
   341→
   342→def _print_code_report(
   343→    code_results: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]],
   344→) -> None:
   345→    """Print the code selection comparison table."""
   346→    print(f"\n{'=' * 80}")
   347→    print("CODE SELECTION VARIANTS (MedGemma prompt changes)")
   348→    print(f"{'=' * 80}")
   349→
   350→    variant_names = list(code_results.keys())
   351→
   352→    # Header
   353→    header = f"{'Entity':<26} {'Expected':<12}"
   354→    for vn in variant_names:
   355→        header += f" {vn:<14}"
   356→    print(header)
   357→    print("-" * len(header))
   358→
   359→    # Rows
   360→    totals = {vn: 0 for vn in variant_names}
   361→    for ent in CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   362→        label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   363→        name = ent["entity_name"][:24]
   364→        expected = ent["expected_code"]
   365→        row = f"{name:<26} {expected:<12}"
   366→        for vn in variant_names:
   367→            got = code_results[vn].get(label, "?")
   368→            match = got == expected
   369→            if match:
   370→                totals[vn] += 1
   371→            marker = "OK" if match else (got or "None")[:12]
   372→            row += f" {marker:<14}"
   373→        print(row)
   374→
   375→    # Totals
   376→    total_ents = len(CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)
   377→    print("-" * len(header))
   378→    totals_row = f"{'Totals:':<26} {'':12}"
   379→    for vn in variant_names:
   380→        totals_row += f" {totals[vn]}/{total_ents:<12}"
   381→    print(totals_row)
   382→
   383→
   384→def _print_relation_report(
   385→    relation_results: dict[str, dict[str, str | None]],
   386→) -> None:
   387→    """Print the relation extraction comparison table."""
   388→    print(f"\n{'=' * 80}")
   389→    print("RELATION EXTRACTION VARIANTS (Field mapper prompt changes)")
   390→    print(f"{'=' * 80}")
   391→
   392→    variant_names = list(relation_results.keys())
   393→
   394→    # Header
   395→    header = f"{'Entity':<26} {'Expected':<12}"
   396→    for vn in variant_names:
   397→        header += f" {vn:<16}"
   398→    print(header)
   399→    print("-" * len(header))
   400→
   401→    # Rows
   402→    totals = {vn: 0 for vn in variant_names}
   403→    for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES:
   404→        label = f"{ent['snippet_idx']}:{ent['entity_idx']}"
   405→        name = ent["entity_name"][:24]
   406→        expected = ent["expected_relation"]
   407→        row = f"{name:<26} {expected:<12}"
   408→        for vn in variant_names:
   409→            got = relation_results[vn].get(label, "?")
   410→            match = got == expected
   411→            if match:
   412→                totals[vn] += 1
   413→            marker = "OK" if match else (got or "None")[:14]
   414→            row += f" {marker:<16}"
   415→        print(row)
   416→
   417→    # Totals
   418→    total_ents = len(RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)
   419→    print("-" * len(header))
   420→    totals_row = f"{'Totals:':<26} {'':12}"
   421→    for vn in variant_names:
   422→        totals_row += f" {totals[vn]}/{total_ents:<14}"
   423→    print(totals_row)
   424→
   425→
   426→# ── Main ────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   427→
   428→
   429→async def main() -> None:
   430→    run_codes = "--relations-only" not in sys.argv
   431→    run_relations = "--codes-only" not in sys.argv
   432→
   433→    data = json.loads(SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())
   434→    snippets = data["grounding_test_snippets"]
   435→
   436→    print("=" * 80)
   437→    print("PROMPT A/B TESTING HARNESS")
   438→    if run_codes:
   439→        print(f"  Code mismatch entities: {len(CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)}")
   440→        print(f"  MedGemma variants: {len(MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS)}")
   441→    if run_relations:
   442→        print(f"  Relation mismatch entities: {len(RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES)}")
   443→        print(f"  Field mapper variants: {len(FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS)}")
   444→    print("=" * 80)
   445→
   446→    start = time.monotonic()
   447→
   448→    code_results: dict = {}
   449→    relation_results: dict = {}
   450→
   451→    # ── Code mismatch tests ──
   452→    if run_codes:
   453→        print("\n[1/2] CODE SELECTION TESTS")
   454→        code_results = await _run_code_mismatch_tests(snippets)
   455→
   456→    # ── Relation mismatch tests ──
   457→    if run_relations:
   458→        print("\n[2/2] RELATION EXTRACTION TESTS")
   459→        relation_results = await _run_relation_mismatch_tests(snippets)
   460→
   461→    elapsed = time.monotonic() - start
   462→
   463→    # ── Reports ──
   464→    if code_results:
   465→        _print_code_report(code_results)
   466→    if relation_results:
   467→        _print_relation_report(relation_results)
   468→
   469→    print(f"\nTotal time: {elapsed:.1f}s")
   470→
   471→    # ── Save JSON results ──
   472→    if os.getenv("SAVE_RESULTS", "1") == "1":
   473→        RESULTS_DIR.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
   474→        output = {
   475→            "code_selection": code_results,
   476→            "relation_extraction": relation_results,
   477→            "entities": {
   478→                "code_mismatch": CODE_MISMATCH_ENTITIES,
   479→                "relation_mismatch": [
   480→                    {
   481→                        k: v
   482→                        for k, v in ent.items()
   483→                        if k
   484→                        not in (
   485→                            "golden_code",
   486→                            "golden_system",
   487→                            "golden_preferred_term",
   488→                        )
   489→                    }
   490→                    for ent in RELATION_MISMATCH_ENTITIES
   491→                ],
   492→            },
   493→            "variants": {
   494→                "medgemma": [v["name"] for v in MEDGEMMA_VARIANTS],
   495→                "field_mapper": [v["name"] for v in FIELD_MAPPER_VARIANTS],
   496→            },
   497→        }
   498→        out_path = RESULTS_DIR / "latest.json"
   499→        out_path.write_text(json.dumps(output, indent=2))
   500→        print(f"Results saved to {out_path}")
   501→
   502→
   503→if __name__ == "__main__":
   504→    asyncio.run(main())
   505→
```

```
     1→#!/usr/bin/env python3
     2→"""Run FULL grounding pipeline against test_snippets.json golden entities.
     3→
     4→Exercises the same code path as the production ground node:
     5→  TerminologyRouter → MedGemma selection → OMOP dual grounding →
     6→  Field mapping generation → Unit normalization
     7→
     8→Usage:
     9→    set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python tests/e2e/run_grounding_snippets.py
    10→"""
    11→
    12→from __future__ import annotations
    13→
    14→import asyncio
    15→import json
    16→import sys
    17→from pathlib import Path
    18→
    19→sys.path.insert(
    20→    0,
    21→    str(
    22→        Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2]
    23→        / "services"
    24→        / "protocol-processor-service"
    25→        / "src"
    26→    ),
    27→)
    28→sys.path.insert(
    29→    0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "services" / "api-service" / "src")
    30→)
    31→sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "libs" / "shared" / "src"))
    32→
    33→from protocol_processor.nodes.ground import _ground_entity_parallel, _get_router
    34→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import normalize_unit
    35→
    36→SNIPPETS_PATH = Path(__file__).parent / "test_snippets.json"
    37→
    38→
    39→async def main() -> None:
    40→    data = json.loads(SNIPPETS_PATH.read_text())
    41→    grounding_snippets = data["grounding_test_snippets"]
    42→
    43→    router = _get_router()
    44→    semaphore = asyncio.Semaphore(4)
    45→
    46→    total_entities = 0
    47→    exact_code_match = 0
    48→    grounded_ok = 0
    49→    unit_ucum_match = 0
    50→    unit_omop_match = 0
    51→    total_units = 0
    52→    field_mapping_count = 0
    53→    results: list[dict] = []
    54→
    55→    for snippet_idx, snippet in enumerate(grounding_snippets):
    56→        snippet_text = snippet["snippet_text"]
    57→        print(f"\n{'─' * 70}")
    58→        print(f"Snippet {snippet_idx}: {snippet_text[:80]}...")
    59→        print(f"{'─' * 70}")
    60→
    61→        for ent_idx, golden in enumerate(snippet["entities"]):
    62→            total_entities += 1
    63→            entity_name = golden["entity_name"]
    64→            expected_code = golden["code"]
    65→            expected_system = golden["system"]
    66→            expected_unit = golden.get("unit")
    67→            expected_ucum = golden.get("unit_ucum")
    68→            expected_omop_cid = golden.get("unit_omop_concept_id")
    69→            expected_relation = golden.get("relation")
    70→            golden.get("value")
    71→
    72→            # Build entity dict matching what parse_node produces
    73→            entity = {
    74→                "text": entity_name,
    75→                "entity_type": "Condition",  # generic — router handles dispatch
    76→                "criterion_text": snippet_text,
    77→                "criteria_type": "inclusion",
    78→            }
    79→
    80→            # Run the FULL grounding pipeline
    81→            result, error, elapsed_ms, retries = await _ground_entity_parallel(
    82→                entity,
    83→                router,
    84→                snippet_text,
    85→                entity_num=ent_idx + 1,
    86→                total=len(snippet["entities"]),
    87→                semaphore=semaphore,
    88→            )
    89→
    90→            if error:
    91→                print(f"\n  ENTITY: {entity_name}")
    92→                print(f"    ERROR: {error}")
    93→                results.append(
    94→                    {"entity": entity_name, "grounded": False, "code_match": False}
    95→                )
    96→                continue
    97→
    98→            if result is None:
    99→                print(f"\n  ENTITY: {entity_name}")
   100→                print("    ERROR: no result and no error (unexpected)")
   101→                results.append(
   102→                    {"entity": entity_name, "grounded": False, "code_match": False}
   103→                )
   104→                continue
   105→
   106→            grounded_ok += 1
   107→            code_match = result.selected_code == expected_code
   108→            if code_match:
   109→                exact_code_match += 1
   110→
   111→            # Check field mappings (narrow type for mypy)
   112→            field_mappings = result.field_mappings or []
   113→            has_mappings = bool(field_mappings)
   114→            if has_mappings:
   115→                field_mapping_count += 1
   116→
   117→            # Print entity result
   118→            status_icon = "OK" if code_match else "MISMATCH"
   119→            print(f"\n  ENTITY: {entity_name}")
   120→            print(
   121→                f"    Code [{status_icon}]: expected={expected_system}:{expected_code}"
   122→                f"  got={result.selected_system}:{result.selected_code}"
   123→            )
   124→            print(f"    Preferred term: {result.preferred_term}")
   125→            print(
   126→                f"    Confidence: {result.confidence:.2f}  |  OMOP: {result.omop_concept_id}"
   127→                f"  |  Retries: {retries}  |  Time: {elapsed_ms:.0f}ms"
   128→            )
   129→            if result.reasoning:
   130→                # Truncate long reasoning
   131→                reasoning_short = result.reasoning[:120] + (
   132→                    "..." if len(result.reasoning) > 120 else ""
   133→                )
   134→                print(f"    Reasoning: {reasoning_short}")
   135→
   136→            # Field mappings
   137→            if has_mappings:
   138→                print(f"    Field mappings ({len(field_mappings)}):")
   139→                for fm in field_mappings:
   140→                    rel = fm.get("relation", "?")
   141→                    val = fm.get("value", {})
   142→                    val_str = val.get("value", val.get("min", "?"))
   143→                    unit = val.get("unit", "")
   144→                    print(
   145→                        f"      {fm.get('entity', '?')} {rel} {val_str} {unit}".rstrip()
   146→                    )
   147→            else:
   148→                print("    Field mappings: NONE")
   149→
   150→            # Check relation and value if golden has them
   151→            if expected_relation and has_mappings:
   152→                got_relation = (
   153→                    field_mappings[0].get("relation") if field_mappings else None
   154→                )
   155→                rel_match = got_relation == expected_relation
   156→                print(
   157→                    f"    Relation: expected={expected_relation}  got={got_relation}"
   158→                    f"  {'OK' if rel_match else 'MISMATCH'}"
   159→                )
   160→
   161→            # Unit normalization
   162→            if expected_unit is not None:
   163→                total_units += 1
   164→                got_ucum, got_omop = normalize_unit(expected_unit)
   165→                ucum_ok = got_ucum == expected_ucum
   166→                omop_ok = got_omop == expected_omop_cid
   167→                if ucum_ok:
   168→                    unit_ucum_match += 1
   169→                if omop_ok:
   170→                    unit_omop_match += 1
   171→                unit_status = "OK" if (ucum_ok and omop_ok) else "MISMATCH"
   172→                print(
   173→                    f"    Unit [{unit_status}]: '{expected_unit}' -> UCUM={got_ucum} OMOP={got_omop}"
   174→                )
   175→
   176→            results.append(
   177→                {
   178→                    "entity": entity_name,
   179→                    "grounded": True,
   180→                    "code_match": code_match,
   181→                    "has_mappings": has_mappings,
   182→                }
   183→            )
   184→
   185→    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   186→    # Summary
   187→    # ═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   188→    print(f"\n{'═' * 70}")
   189→    print("FULL PIPELINE RESULTS")
   190→    print(f"{'═' * 70}")
   191→    print(f"  Entities:            {total_entities}")
   192→    print(
   193→        f"  Grounded:            {grounded_ok}/{total_entities}"
   194→        f" ({100 * grounded_ok // max(total_entities, 1)}%)"
   195→    )
   196→    print(
   197→        f"  Exact code match:    {exact_code_match}/{total_entities}"
   198→        f" ({100 * exact_code_match // max(total_entities, 1)}%)"
   199→    )
   200→    print(
   201→        f"  With field mappings: {field_mapping_count}/{total_entities}"
   202→        f" ({100 * field_mapping_count // max(total_entities, 1)}%)"
   203→    )
   204→    print(
   205→        f"  Unit UCUM match:     {unit_ucum_match}/{total_units}"
   206→        f" ({100 * unit_ucum_match // max(total_units, 1)}%)"
   207→    )
   208→    print(
   209→        f"  Unit OMOP match:     {unit_omop_match}/{total_units}"
   210→        f" ({100 * unit_omop_match // max(total_units, 1)}%)"
   211→    )
   212→    print(f"{'═' * 70}")
   213→
   214→    # Failures summary
   215→    failures = [r for r in results if not r.get("code_match")]
   216→    if failures:
   217→        print("\nCode match failures:")
   218→        for f in failures:
   219→            status = "ERROR" if not f["grounded"] else "MISMATCH"
   220→            print(f"  [{status}] {f['entity']}")
   221→
   222→    no_mappings = [
   223→        r for r in results if r.get("grounded") and not r.get("has_mappings")
   224→    ]
   225→    if no_mappings:
   226→        print("\nMissing field mappings:")
   227→        for f in no_mappings:
   228→            print(f"  {f['entity']}")
   229→
   230→
   231→if __name__ == "__main__":
```

```
     1→"""MLflow tracing helpers for pipeline nodes.
     2→
     3→Provides a safe context manager that creates MLflow traces when available
     4→and falls back to a no-op when MLflow is not configured or installed.
     5→
     6→Each node creates its own separate top-level trace (not a child span),
     7→tagged with protocol_id and run_id so traces from the same pipeline run
     8→can be grouped and filtered in the MLflow UI.
     9→
    10→IMPORTANT: ``mlflow.langchain.autolog()`` must NOT be enabled.  Autolog
    11→wraps ``graph.ainvoke()`` in a single parent trace and all node spans
    12→become children — producing one constantly-updating trace that only
    13→appears after the full pipeline finishes.  Without autolog, each call to
    14→``mlflow.start_span()`` inside a node creates an independent root trace
    15→that is flushed to MLflow as soon as the context manager exits.
    16→
    17→Grouping tags set on every trace:
    18→    protocol_id  – groups all runs for a given protocol
    19→    run_id       – groups all node traces from the same pipeline invocation
    20→    node         – the node name (for easy filtering)
    21→"""
    22→
    23→from __future__ import annotations
    24→
    25→import contextvars
    26→import logging
    27→import os
    28→from contextlib import contextmanager
    29→from typing import Any, Generator, cast
    30→
    31→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    32→
    33→# ContextVar set once per pipeline invocation in trigger._run_pipeline().
    34→# Every node's pipeline_span() reads it to tag traces with a shared run_id.
    35→_pipeline_run_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str] = contextvars.ContextVar(
    36→    "pipeline_run_id", default=""
    37→)
    38→
    39→# Experiment ID for pipeline traces. Set by trigger after _ensure_mlflow() so
    40→# spans carry the experiment ID; export then uses it even from another thread.
    41→_pipeline_experiment_id: contextvars.ContextVar[str | None] = contextvars.ContextVar(
    42→    "pipeline_experiment_id", default=None
    43→)
    44→
    45→
    46→def set_pipeline_run_id(run_id: str) -> None:
    47→    """Set the current pipeline run_id (call before graph.ainvoke)."""
    48→    _pipeline_run_id.set(run_id)
    49→
    50→
    51→def set_pipeline_experiment_id(experiment_id: str | None) -> None:
    52→    """Set experiment ID for pipeline traces (after _ensure_mlflow in trigger)."""
    53→    _pipeline_experiment_id.set(experiment_id)
    54→
    55→
    56→@contextmanager
    57→def pipeline_span(
    58→    name: str,
    59→    span_type: str = "CHAIN",
    60→    protocol_id: str = "",
    61→) -> Generator[Any, None, None]:
    62→    """Create a separate MLflow trace for a pipeline node.
    63→
    64→    Each call creates its own top-level trace tagged with protocol_id
    65→    and run_id, so individual node traces appear in MLflow as they
    66→    complete rather than waiting for the entire pipeline to finish.
    67→
    68→    Filter in MLflow UI:
    69→        tags.protocol_id = "<id>"       – all runs for a protocol
    70→        tags.run_id      = "<run_id>"   – single pipeline invocation
    71→
    72→    Args:
    73→        name: Trace/span name (e.g., "ingest_node", "ground_node").
    74→        span_type: MLflow span type (default "CHAIN").
    75→        protocol_id: Protocol ID to tag the trace with for session grouping.
    76→
    77→    Yields:
    78→        MLflow Span object or a no-op wrapper.
    79→    """
    80→    try:
    81→        import mlflow
    82→        from mlflow.entities.trace_location import MlflowExperimentLocation
    83→
    84→        if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
    85→            # Pass experiment_id on span so export uses it even from another thread.
    86→            exp_id = _pipeline_experiment_id.get()
    87→            trace_destination = (
    88→                MlflowExperimentLocation(experiment_id=exp_id) if exp_id else None
    89→            )
    90→            with mlflow.start_span(
    91→                name=name,
    92→                span_type=span_type,
    93→                trace_destination=trace_destination,
    94→            ) as span:
    95→                # Tag the trace for grouping/filtering in the MLflow UI.
    96→                tags: dict[str, str] = {"node": name}
    97→                if protocol_id:
    98→                    tags["protocol_id"] = protocol_id
    99→                run_id = _pipeline_run_id.get()
   100→                if run_id:
   101→                    tags["run_id"] = run_id
   102→                try:
   103→                    mlflow.update_current_trace(tags=tags)
   104→                except Exception:
   105→                    logger.warning("MLflow tag update failed", exc_info=True)
   106→                yield cast(Any, span)
   107→                return
   108→    except ImportError:
   109→        logger.warning("mlflow not installed — tracing disabled")
   110→    except Exception:
   111→        logger.warning(
   112→            "MLflow span creation failed, falling back to no-op", exc_info=True
   113→        )
   114→
   115→    # Fallback: no-op span
   116→    yield cast(Any, _NoOpSpan())
   117→
   118→
   119→class _NoOpSpan:
   120→    """No-op span that silently accepts MLflow span API calls."""
   121→
   122→    def set_inputs(self, inputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   123→        pass
   124→
   125→    def set_outputs(self, outputs: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
   126→        pass
   127→
   128→    def set_status(self, status: str) -> None:
   129→        pass
   130→
   131→
   132→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   133→# LLM-level child span helpers
   134→# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
   135→
   136→_TRUNCATE_LEN = 10_000
   137→
   138→
   139→def _truncate(text: str, max_len: int = _TRUNCATE_LEN) -> str:
   140→    if len(text) <= max_len:
   141→        return text
   142→    return text[:max_len] + f"... [truncated, {len(text)} total chars]"
   143→
   144→
   145→class _LLMSpanCtx:
   146→    """Wraps an MLflow span and exposes set_request / set_response helpers."""
   147→
   148→    def __init__(self, span: Any, model_name: str) -> None:
   149→        self._span = span
   150→        self._model_name = model_name
   151→        self._finalized = False
   152→
   153→    def set_request(self, prompt_text: str) -> None:
   154→        """Record the LLM request (prompt + model) as span inputs."""
   155→        self._span.set_inputs(
   156→            {
   157→                "model": self._model_name,
   158→                "prompt": _truncate(prompt_text),
   159→            }
   160→        )
   161→
   162→    def set_response(
   163→        self,
   164→        response_text: str,
   165→        usage: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   166→    ) -> None:
   167→        """Record the LLM response and optional token counts as span outputs."""
   168→        outputs: dict[str, Any] = {"response": _truncate(response_text)}
   169→        if usage:
   170→            outputs.update(usage)
   171→        self._span.set_outputs(outputs)
   172→        self._finalized = True
   173→
   174→    def _finalize(self) -> None:
   175→        """Ensure outputs are set even if set_response was never called."""
   176→        if not self._finalized:
   177→            self._span.set_outputs({"response": "(not captured)"})
   178→
   179→
   180→class _NoOpLLMSpan:
   181→    """No-op replacement when MLflow is unavailable."""
   182→
   183→    def set_request(self, prompt_text: str) -> None:
   184→        pass
   185→
   186→    def set_response(
   187→        self,
   188→        response_text: str,
   189→        usage: dict[str, int] | None = None,
   190→    ) -> None:
   191→        pass
   192→
   193→
   194→@contextmanager
   195→def llm_span(name: str, model_name: str = "") -> Generator[Any, None, None]:
   196→    """Create an MLflow child span for an individual LLM call.
   197→
   198→    When called inside an active ``pipeline_span()``, this automatically
   199→    becomes a child span (MLflow 3.x nests ``start_span`` calls).
   200→    When MLflow is unavailable the context manager yields a no-op object.
   201→
   202→    Args:
   203→        name: Span name (e.g. ``"gemini_extraction"``).
   204→        model_name: Model identifier to record in span inputs.
   205→
   206→    Yields:
   207→        ``_LLMSpanCtx`` (or ``_NoOpLLMSpan``) with ``set_request`` /
   208→        ``set_response`` helpers.
   209→    """
   210→    try:
   211→        import mlflow
   212→
   213→        if os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"):
   214→            with mlflow.start_span(name=name, span_type="LLM") as span:
   215→                ctx = _LLMSpanCtx(span, model_name)
   216→                yield cast(Any, ctx)
   217→                ctx._finalize()
   218→                return
   219→    except ImportError:
   220→        pass  # mlflow is an optional dependency; fall through to no-op span
   221→    except Exception:
   222→        logger.debug("llm_span creation failed, falling back to no-op", exc_info=True)
   223→
   224→    yield cast(Any, _NoOpLLMSpan())
   225→
```

```
     1→"""MedGemma decision tool for best-match selection from TerminologyRouter candidates.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "MedGemma acts as decision-maker, minimum token usage."
     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.
     7→"""
     8→
     9→from __future__ import annotations
    10→
    11→import logging
    12→import os
    13→from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any
    14→
    15→if TYPE_CHECKING:
    16→    from protocol_processor.tools.terminology_router import TerminologyRouter
    17→
    18→from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
    19→from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
    20→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field
    21→
    22→from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template
    23→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import (
    24→    EntityGroundingResult,
    25→    GroundingCandidate,
    26→)
    27→
    28→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    29→
    30→_model_loader = None
    31→
    32→
    33→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    34→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    35→
    36→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    37→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    38→
    39→    Attributes:
    40→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    41→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    42→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    43→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    44→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    45→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    46→    """
    47→
    48→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    49→        default=False,
    50→        description=(
    51→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    52→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    53→        ),
    54→    )
    55→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    56→        default=False,
    57→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    58→    )
    59→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None,
    61→        description=(
    62→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    63→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    64→        ),
    65→    )
    66→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    67→        default=None,
    68→        description=(
    69→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    70→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    71→        ),
    72→    )
    73→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    74→        default=None,
    75→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),
    76→    )
    77→    reasoning: str = Field(
    78→        default="",
    79→        description="Brief explanation of the three-question reasoning",
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
    84→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
    85→
    86→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
    87→        default=None,
    88→        description=(
    89→            "Selected terminology code (CUI for UMLS, SNOMED code, etc)."
    90→            " Null if no good match."
    91→        ),
    92→    )
    93→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
    94→        default=None,
    95→        description=(
    96→            "The API/system that produced the selected code (e.g. 'umls', 'snomed')."
    97→        ),
    98→    )
    99→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
   100→        default=None,
   101→        description="Canonical preferred term for the selected code.",
   102→    )
   103→    confidence: float = Field(
   104→        ge=0.0,
   105→        le=1.0,
   106→        description=(
   107→            "Confidence score 0.0-1.0."
   108→            " 0.9-1.0=exact, 0.7-0.8=synonym, 0.5-0.6=partial, 0.0=no match."
   109→        ),
   110→    )
   111→    reasoning: str = Field(
   112→        default="",
   113→        description="Brief explanation for selection.",
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   118→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   119→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   120→    if _model_loader is None:
   121→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   122→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   123→
   124→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   125→    return _model_loader()
   126→
   127→
   128→async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   129→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   130→
   131→    Args:
   132→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   133→
   134→    Returns:
   135→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   136→    """
   137→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   138→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   139→
   140→    if not google_api_key:
   141→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   142→
   143→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   144→
   145→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   146→        model=gemini_model_name,
   147→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   148→        max_output_tokens=2048,
   149→    )
   150→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   151→
   152→    prompt = render_template("structure_decision.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text)
   153→
   154→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_decision", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   155→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   156→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   157→        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)
   158→        llm.set_response(resp_text)
   159→
   160→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   161→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   162→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   163→
   164→
   165→async def medgemma_decide(
   166→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   167→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   168→    criterion_context: str,
   169→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   170→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   171→
   172→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   173→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   174→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   175→    appropriate.
   176→
   177→    Args:
   178→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   179→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   180→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   181→
   182→    Returns:
   183→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   184→    """
   185→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   186→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   187→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   188→
   189→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   190→    if not candidates:
   191→        logger.info(
   192→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   193→            " — returning empty grounding",
   194→            entity_text[:50],
   195→            entity_type,
   196→            criterion_id[:12],
   197→        )
   198→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   199→            entity_text=entity_text,
   200→            entity_type=entity_type,
   201→            selected_code=None,
   202→            selected_system=None,
   203→            preferred_term=None,
   204→            confidence=0.0,
   205→            candidates=[],
   206→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   207→        )
   208→
   209→    try:
   210→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   211→
   212→        system_prompt = render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   213→        evaluate_prompt = render_template(
   214→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   215→            entity_text=entity_text,
   216→            entity_type=entity_type,
   217→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   218→            candidates=candidates,
   219→        )
   220→
   221→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   222→
   223→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   224→        messages = [
   225→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   226→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   227→        ]
   228→
   229→        with llm_span("medgemma_evaluate", str(model_name)) as llm:
   230→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {evaluate_prompt}")
   231→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   232→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   233→            usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   234→            um = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   235→            if isinstance(um, dict):
   236→                if um.get("input_tokens"):
   237→                    usage["input_tokens"] = um["input_tokens"]
   238→                if um.get("output_tokens"):
   239→                    usage["output_tokens"] = um["output_tokens"]
   240→                if um.get("total_tokens"):
   241→                    usage["total_tokens"] = um["total_tokens"]
   242→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage or None)
   243→
   244→        logger.debug(
   245→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   246→            entity_text[:30],
   247→            raw_text[:200],
   248→        )
   249→
   250→        decision = await _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   251→
   252→        logger.info(
   253→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   254→            entity_text[:50],
   255→            decision.selected_code,
   256→            decision.selected_system,
   257→            decision.confidence,
   258→        )
   259→
   260→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   261→            entity_text=entity_text,
   262→            entity_type=entity_type,
   263→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   264→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   265→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   266→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   267→            candidates=candidates,
   268→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   269→        )
   270→
   271→    except Exception as e:
   272→        logger.error(
   273→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   274→            entity_text[:50],
   275→            e,
   276→            exc_info=True,
   277→        )
   278→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   279→            entity_text=entity_text,
   280→            entity_type=entity_type,
   281→            selected_code=None,
   282→            selected_system=None,
   283→            preferred_term=None,
   284→            confidence=0.0,
   285→            candidates=candidates,
   286→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   291→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   292→
   293→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   294→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   295→    the output.
   296→
   297→    Args:
   298→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   299→
   300→    Returns:
   301→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   302→    """
   303→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   304→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   305→
   306→    if not google_api_key:
   307→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   308→
   309→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   310→
   311→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   312→        model=gemini_model_name,
   313→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   314→        max_output_tokens=2048,
   315→    )
   316→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   317→
   318→    prompt = render_template("structure_reasoning.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text)
   319→
   320→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_reasoning", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   321→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   322→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   323→        llm.set_response(str(result))
   324→
   325→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   326→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   327→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   328→
   329→
   330→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   331→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   332→    criterion_context: str,
   333→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   334→    attempt: int = 1,
   335→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   336→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   337→
   338→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   339→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   340→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   341→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   342→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   343→
   344→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   345→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   346→
   347→    Args:
   348→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   349→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   350→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   351→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   352→
   353→    Returns:
   354→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   355→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   356→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   357→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   358→    """
   359→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   360→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   361→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   362→
   363→    logger.info(
   364→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   365→        attempt,
   366→        entity_text[:50],
   367→        entity_type,
   368→    )
   369→
   370→    try:
   371→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   372→
   373→        system_prompt = render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   374→        reasoning_prompt = render_template(
   375→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   376→            entity_text=entity_text,
   377→            entity_type=entity_type,
   378→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   379→            previous_query=previous_query,
   380→            attempt=attempt,
   381→        )
   382→
   383→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   384→
   385→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   386→        messages = [
   387→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   388→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   389→        ]
   390→
   391→        with llm_span("medgemma_reasoning", str(model_name)) as llm:
   392→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {reasoning_prompt}")
   393→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   394→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   395→            usage_r: dict[str, int] = {}
   396→            um_r = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   397→            if isinstance(um_r, dict):
   398→                if um_r.get("input_tokens"):
   399→                    usage_r["input_tokens"] = um_r["input_tokens"]
   400→                if um_r.get("output_tokens"):
   401→                    usage_r["output_tokens"] = um_r["output_tokens"]
   402→                if um_r.get("total_tokens"):
   403→                    usage_r["total_tokens"] = um_r["total_tokens"]
   404→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage_r or None)
   405→
   406→        logger.debug(
   407→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   408→            entity_text[:30],
   409→            raw_text[:300],
   410→        )
   411→
   412→        result = await _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   413→
   414→        logger.info(
   415→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   416→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   417→            entity_text[:50],
   418→            result.should_skip,
   419→            result.is_derived,
   420→            result.derived_term,
   421→            result.rephrased_query,
   422→        )
   423→
   424→        return result
   425→
   426→    except Exception as e:
   427→        logger.error(
   428→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   429→            entity_text[:50],
   430→            e,
   431→            exc_info=True,
   432→        )
   433→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   434→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   435→            should_skip=False,
   436→            is_derived=False,
   437→            derived_term=None,
   438→            rephrased_query=None,
   439→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   440→        )
   441→
```
