     1→"""Structure builder tool for expression tree construction.
     2→
     3→Phase 2: Detects AND/OR/NOT logic in criterion text using Gemini,
     4→then builds normalized atomic_criteria/composite_criteria records
     5→and returns a StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage.
     6→
     7→Follows the field_mapper.py pattern: Gemini structured output with
     8→graceful fallback on failure.
     9→"""
    10→
    11→from __future__ import annotations
    12→
    13→import logging
    14→import os
    15→from typing import Any
    16→
    17→from shared.models import AtomicCriterion, CompositeCriterion, CriterionRelationship
    18→from sqlmodel import Session
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template
    21→from protocol_processor.schemas.structure import (
    22→    ExpressionNode,
    23→    LogicDetectionResponse,
    24→    LogicNode,
    25→    StructuredCriterionTree,
    26→)
    27→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    28→    create_structured_llm,
    29→    parse_structured_output,
    30→)
    31→from protocol_processor.tools.unit_normalizer import (
    32→    normalize_ordinal_value,
    33→    normalize_unit,
    34→    normalize_value,
    35→)
    36→
    37→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    38→
    39→# Map pipeline entity_type to OMOP-style entity_domain.
    40→# Used when field_mappings don't include an explicit entity_domain.
    41→_ENTITY_TYPE_TO_DOMAIN: dict[str, str] = {
    42→    "Condition": "condition",
    43→    "Medication": "drug",
    44→    "Lab_Value": "measurement",
    45→    "Procedure": "procedure",
    46→    "Demographic": "demographics",
    47→    "Other": "observation",
    48→}
    49→
    50→
    51→def _extract_value_fields(fm: dict[str, Any]) -> tuple[str, str | None]:
    52→    """Extract flat value string and unit from a field mapping.
    53→
    54→    Handles both legacy flat strings and typed value dicts produced by
    55→    ``FieldMappingValue.model_dump(exclude_none=True)``.
    56→
    57→    Args:
    58→        fm: Field mapping dict with ``value`` (str or dict) and optional ``unit``.
    59→
    60→    Returns:
    61→        Tuple of (value_str, unit) where value_str is always a string
    62→        (possibly empty) and unit is the resolved unit string or None.
    63→    """
    64→    raw_value = fm.get("value", "")
    65→    top_unit = fm.get("unit")
    66→
    67→    if isinstance(raw_value, dict):
    68→        vtype = raw_value.get("type", "standard")
    69→        unit = raw_value.get("unit") or top_unit
    70→
    71→        if vtype == "range":
    72→            v_min = raw_value.get("min", "")
    73→            v_max = raw_value.get("max", "")
    74→            if v_min and v_max:
    75→                return f"{v_min}-{v_max}", unit
    76→            return v_min or v_max or "", unit
    77→        elif vtype == "temporal":
    78→            return raw_value.get("duration", ""), unit
    79→        else:  # standard
    80→            return raw_value.get("value", ""), unit
    81→
    82→    # Legacy: flat string
    83→    if not isinstance(raw_value, str):
    84→        raw_value = str(raw_value) if raw_value is not None else ""
    85→    return raw_value, top_unit
    86→
    87→
    88→def _parse_value(
    89→    raw_value: str,
    90→) -> tuple[float | None, str | None]:
    91→    """Parse a value string into numeric or text.
    92→
    93→    Tries float conversion first. If it succeeds, returns (numeric, None).
    94→    Otherwise returns (None, text).
    95→
    96→    Args:
    97→        raw_value: Raw value string from field_mapping.
    98→
    99→    Returns:
   100→        Tuple of (value_numeric, value_text) — one is always None.
   101→    """
   102→    try:
   103→        return (float(raw_value), None)
   104→    except (ValueError, TypeError):
   105→        return (None, raw_value)
   106→
   107→
   108→async def detect_logic_structure(
   109→    criterion_text: str,
   110→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   111→) -> LogicDetectionResponse | None:
   112→    """Detect AND/OR/NOT logic structure in criterion text using Gemini.
   113→
   114→    Skips LLM call if there's only 1 field_mapping (single atom, no logic).
   115→    Returns None on any failure (triggers fallback in caller).
   116→
   117→    Args:
   118→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   119→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   120→
   121→    Returns:
   122→        LogicDetectionResponse with the detected tree, or None on failure.
   123→    """
   124→    if len(field_mappings) <= 1:
   125→        return None
   126→
   127→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(LogicDetectionResponse)
   128→    if structured_llm is None:
   129→        return None
   130→
   131→    try:
   132→        # Build field mapping descriptions for context
   133→        mapping_lines = []
   134→        for i, fm in enumerate(field_mappings):
   135→            entity = fm.get("entity", "?")
   136→            relation = fm.get("relation", "?")
   137→            val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   138→            value = val_str or "?"
   139→            unit_suffix = f" {unit_str}" if unit_str else ""
   140→            mapping_lines.append(f"  [{i}] {entity} {relation} {value}{unit_suffix}")
   141→        mappings_text = "\n".join(mapping_lines)
   142→
   143→        prompt = render_template(
   144→            "logic_detection.jinja2",
   145→            criterion_text=criterion_text,
   146→            mappings_text=mappings_text,
   147→            max_index=len(field_mappings) - 1,
   148→        )
   149→
   150→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   151→
   152→        with llm_span("gemini_logic_detection") as llm:
   153→            llm.set_request(prompt)
   154→            result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   155→            llm.set_response(str(result))
   156→
   157→        response = parse_structured_output(result, LogicDetectionResponse)
   158→
   159→        # Validate all field_mapping_index values are in range
   160→        if not _validate_logic_tree(response.root, len(field_mappings)):
   161→            logger.warning(
   162→                "Logic tree validation failed for '%s' — invalid indices",
   163→                criterion_text[:50],
   164→            )
   165→            return None
   166→
   167→        return response
   168→
   169→    except Exception as e:
   170→        logger.warning(
   171→            "Logic detection failed for '%s': %s",
   172→            criterion_text[:50],
   173→            e,
   174→            exc_info=True,
   175→        )
   176→        return None
   177→
   178→
   179→def _validate_logic_tree(node: LogicNode, num_mappings: int) -> bool:
   180→    """Validate that all field_mapping_index values are in range.
   181→
   182→    Args:
   183→        node: Root LogicNode to validate.
   184→        num_mappings: Number of field_mappings (valid range: 0..num_mappings-1).
   185→
   186→    Returns:
   187→        True if all indices are valid, False otherwise.
   188→    """
   189→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   190→        if node.field_mapping_index is None:
   191→            return False
   192→        return 0 <= node.field_mapping_index < num_mappings
   193→    if node.children:
   194→        return all(_validate_logic_tree(child, num_mappings) for child in node.children)
   195→    # Non-ATOMIC with no children is invalid (AND/OR/NOT need children)
   196→    return False
   197→
   198→
   199→def _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   200→    fm: dict[str, Any],
   201→    criterion_id: str,
   202→    protocol_id: str,
   203→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   204→    criterion_text: str,
   205→) -> AtomicCriterion:
   206→    """Create an AtomicCriterion record from a field_mapping dict.
   207→
   208→    Args:
   209→        fm: Field mapping dict with entity, relation, value, unit keys.
   210→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   211→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   212→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   213→        criterion_text: Original criterion text.
   214→
   215→    Returns:
   216→        AtomicCriterion instance (not yet added to session).
   217→    """
   218→    value_str, raw_unit = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   219→    value_numeric, value_text = _parse_value(value_str)
   220→
   221→    relation = fm.get("relation", "has")
   222→    negation = relation.upper() == "NOT" if relation else False
   223→
   224→    ucum_code, unit_concept_id = normalize_unit(raw_unit)
   225→
   226→    value_concept_id: int | None = None
   227→    entity_text = fm.get("entity")
   228→
   229→    # Ordinal-first: check if entity is an ordinal scale
   230→    ordinal_result = normalize_ordinal_value(value_str, entity_text)
   231→    if ordinal_result is not None:
   232→        ordinal_value_cid, ordinal_unit_cid = ordinal_result
   233→        value_concept_id = ordinal_value_cid
   234→        if ordinal_unit_cid is not None:
   235→            unit_concept_id = ordinal_unit_cid
   236→            ucum_code = "{score}"
   237→    elif value_text and value_numeric is None:
   238→        _, value_concept_id = normalize_value(value_text)
   239→
   240→    # Derive entity_domain from entity_type when not explicitly provided
   241→    entity_domain = fm.get("entity_domain")
   242→    if not entity_domain:
   243→        entity_type = fm.get("entity_type", "")
   244→        entity_domain = _ENTITY_TYPE_TO_DOMAIN.get(entity_type)
   245→
   246→    return AtomicCriterion(
   247→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   248→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   249→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   250→        entity_concept_id=fm.get("entity_concept_id"),
   251→        entity_concept_system=fm.get("entity_concept_system"),
   252→        omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   253→        entity_domain=entity_domain,
   254→        relation_operator=relation,
   255→        value_numeric=value_numeric,
   256→        value_text=value_text,
   257→        unit_text=raw_unit,
   258→        unit_ucum_code=ucum_code,
   259→        unit_concept_id=unit_concept_id,
   260→        value_concept_id=value_concept_id,
   261→        negation=negation,
   262→        original_text=criterion_text,
   263→        confidence_score=fm.get("confidence_score"),
   264→    )
   265→
   266→
   267→def _build_tree_from_logic(
   268→    node: LogicNode,
   269→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   270→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion],
   271→    criterion_id: str,
   272→    protocol_id: str,
   273→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   274→    session: Session,
   275→) -> tuple[ExpressionNode, str]:
   276→    """Recursively build ExpressionNode tree and DB records from LogicNode.
   277→
   278→    Creates CompositeCriterion and CriterionRelationship records for
   279→    branch nodes. Leaf nodes reference already-created AtomicCriterion
   280→    records by index.
   281→
   282→    Args:
   283→        node: Current LogicNode from LLM output.
   284→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts.
   285→        atomic_records: Pre-created AtomicCriterion records (indexed).
   286→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   287→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   288→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   289→        session: Active SQLModel session.
   290→
   291→    Returns:
   292→        Tuple of (ExpressionNode for JSONB storage, node's DB record ID).
   293→    """
   294→    if node.node_type == "ATOMIC":
   295→        idx = node.field_mapping_index or 0
   296→        atomic = atomic_records[idx]
   297→        fm = field_mappings[idx]
   298→        val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   299→        expr = ExpressionNode(
   300→            type="ATOMIC",
   301→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   302→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   303→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   304→            value=val_str or None,
   305→            unit=unit_str,
   306→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   307→        )
   308→        return expr, atomic.id
   309→
   310→    # Branch node: create CompositeCriterion
   311→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   312→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   313→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   314→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   315→        logic_operator=node.node_type,
   316→    )
   317→    session.add(composite)
   318→    session.flush()
   319→
   320→    children_nodes: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   321→    for seq, child in enumerate(node.children or []):
   322→        child_expr, child_db_id = _build_tree_from_logic(
   323→            child,
   324→            field_mappings,
   325→            atomic_records,
   326→            criterion_id,
   327→            protocol_id,
   328→            inclusion_exclusion,
   329→            session,
   330→        )
   331→        children_nodes.append(child_expr)
   332→
   333→        child_type = "atomic" if child.node_type == "ATOMIC" else "composite"
   334→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   335→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   336→            child_criterion_id=child_db_id,
   337→            child_type=child_type,
   338→            child_sequence=seq,
   339→        )
   340→        session.add(rel)
   341→
   342→    expr = ExpressionNode(
   343→        type=node.node_type,
   344→        children=children_nodes,
   345→    )
   346→    return expr, composite.id
   347→
   348→
   349→async def build_expression_tree(
   350→    criterion_text: str,
   351→    field_mappings: list[dict[str, Any]],
   352→    criterion_id: str,
   353→    protocol_id: str,
   354→    inclusion_exclusion: str,
   355→    session: Session,
   356→) -> StructuredCriterionTree:
   357→    """Build an expression tree from criterion field_mappings.
   358→
   359→    Two-pass approach:
   360→    1. LLM pass: call detect_logic_structure() for AND/OR/NOT detection
   361→    2. Fallback: if LLM fails or single mapping, AND-of-all-atomics
   362→
   363→    Creates AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping, plus
   364→    CompositeCriterion + CriterionRelationship for tree structure.
   365→
   366→    Args:
   367→        criterion_text: Full criterion text.
   368→        field_mappings: List of field_mapping dicts from conditions.
   369→        criterion_id: FK to criteria table.
   370→        protocol_id: FK to protocol table.
   371→        inclusion_exclusion: "inclusion" or "exclusion".
   372→        session: Active SQLModel session (records are added but not committed).
   373→
   374→    Returns:
   375→        StructuredCriterionTree for JSONB storage in criteria.structured_criterion.
   376→    """
   377→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   378→
   379→    # Create AtomicCriterion records for each field_mapping
   380→    atomic_records: list[AtomicCriterion] = []
   381→    for fm in field_mappings:
   382→        atomic = _create_atomic_from_mapping(
   383→            fm, criterion_id, protocol_id, inclusion_exclusion, criterion_text
   384→        )
   385→        session.add(atomic)
   386→        session.flush()
   387→        atomic_records.append(atomic)
   388→
   389→    # Pass 1: LLM logic detection
   390→    logic_response = await detect_logic_structure(criterion_text, field_mappings)
   391→
   392→    if logic_response is not None:
   393→        # Build tree from LLM-detected structure
   394→        root_expr, _ = _build_tree_from_logic(
   395→            logic_response.root,
   396→            field_mappings,
   397→            atomic_records,
   398→            criterion_id,
   399→            protocol_id,
   400→            inclusion_exclusion,
   401→            session,
   402→        )
   403→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   404→            root=root_expr,
   405→            structure_confidence="llm",
   406→            structure_model=gemini_model_name,
   407→        )
   408→
   409→    # Pass 2: Fallback — AND-of-all-atomics (or single ATOMIC)
   410→    if len(atomic_records) == 1:
   411→        fm = field_mappings[0]
   412→        val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   413→        root_expr = ExpressionNode(
   414→            type="ATOMIC",
   415→            atomic_criterion_id=atomic_records[0].id,
   416→            entity=fm.get("entity"),
   417→            relation=fm.get("relation"),
   418→            value=val_str or None,
   419→            unit=unit_str,
   420→            omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   421→        )
   422→        return StructuredCriterionTree(
   423→            root=root_expr,
   424→            structure_confidence="fallback",
   425→            structure_model=None,
   426→        )
   427→
   428→    # Multiple mappings, LLM failed → wrap in AND
   429→    composite = CompositeCriterion(
   430→        criterion_id=criterion_id,
   431→        protocol_id=protocol_id,
   432→        inclusion_exclusion=inclusion_exclusion,
   433→        logic_operator="AND",
   434→        original_text=criterion_text,
   435→    )
   436→    session.add(composite)
   437→    session.flush()
   438→
   439→    children: list[ExpressionNode] = []
   440→    for seq, (atomic, fm) in enumerate(zip(atomic_records, field_mappings)):
   441→        val_str, unit_str = _extract_value_fields(fm)
   442→        children.append(
   443→            ExpressionNode(
   444→                type="ATOMIC",
   445→                atomic_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   446→                entity=fm.get("entity"),
   447→                relation=fm.get("relation"),
   448→                value=val_str or None,
   449→                unit=unit_str,
   450→                omop_concept_id=fm.get("omop_concept_id"),
   451→            )
   452→        )
   453→        rel = CriterionRelationship(
   454→            parent_criterion_id=composite.id,
   455→            child_criterion_id=atomic.id,
   456→            child_type="atomic",
   457→            child_sequence=seq,
   458→        )
   459→        session.add(rel)
   460→
   461→    root_expr = ExpressionNode(type="AND", children=children)
   462→    return StructuredCriterionTree(
   463→        root=root_expr,
   464→        structure_confidence="fallback",
   465→        structure_model=None,
   466→    )
   467→
```

> AGENT

Good. The field mapper is the primary risk since it outputs free-form strings into the UI. The other two (ordinal_resolver, structure_builder) produce more constrained schemas. Let me implement the fix in two files.

**Step 1**: Add a `RepetitionLoopError` and a recursive model checker to `gemini_utils.py`.

> TOOL

```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/gemini_utils.py",
  "old_string": "T = TypeVar(\"T\", bound=BaseModel)",
  "new_string": "class RepetitionLoopError(RuntimeError):\n    \"\"\"Raised when a Gemini structured-output response contains repetition artifacts.\"\"\"\n\n\ndef check_model_for_repetition(model: BaseModel) -> list[str]:\n    \"\"\"Recursively inspect all string fields of a Pydantic model for repetition.\n\n    Returns a list of field paths that contain repetition artifacts,\n    e.g. [\"mappings[0].value.unit\", \"mappings[1].value.value\"].\n    \"\"\"\n    bad_fields: list[str] = []\n\n    def _check(obj: Any, prefix: str) -> None:\n        if isinstance(obj, BaseModel):\n            for field_name in obj.model_fields:\n                _check(getattr(obj, field_name, None), f\"{prefix}.{field_name}\")\n        elif isinstance(obj, list):\n            for i, item in enumerate(obj):\n                _check(item, f\"{prefix}[{i}]\")\n        elif isinstance(obj, str) and is_repetition_loop(obj):\n            bad_fields.append(prefix)\n\n    _check(model, \"root\")\n    return bad_fields\n\n\nT = TypeVar(\"T\", bound=BaseModel)"
}

     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→
     7→Falls back to Ollama when GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set but OLLAMA_BASE_URL
     8→is configured.
     9→
    10→Includes detection for a known Gemini structured-output bug where the
    11→model enters a token repetition loop, producing repeating digits or
    12→regurgitated schema descriptions instead of actual values.  See:
    13→  - https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449
    14→  - https://github.com/googleapis/python-genai/issues/1039
    15→"""
    16→
    17→from __future__ import annotations
    18→
    19→import logging
    20→import os
    21→import re
    22→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    23→
    24→from pydantic import BaseModel
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Pattern that detects schema description regurgitation — the model
    29→# outputs Pydantic field descriptions instead of actual values.
    30→_SCHEMA_LEAK_RE = re.compile(
    31→    r"(?:Unit of measurement|Value for standard type|Duration value for)",
    32→    re.IGNORECASE,
    33→)
    34→
    35→
    36→def is_repetition_loop(text: str | None) -> bool:
    37→    """Detect if text contains a Gemini repetition loop.
    38→
    39→    Known Gemini bug: the model enters a token repetition loop during
    40→    structured output generation, producing sequences like
    41→    ``"220220220220220220"`` or ``"8598739459392231e-315"`` repeated
    42→    until ``max_output_tokens`` is exhausted.
    43→
    44→    Returns True if the text is likely a repetition artifact.
    45→    """
    46→    if not text or len(text) < 20:
    47→        return False
    48→
    49→    # Check 1: very low character diversity for the string length
    50→    unique_ratio = len(set(text)) / len(text)
    51→    if unique_ratio < 0.08 and len(text) > 30:
    52→        return True
    53→
    54→    # Check 2: short repeating substring (3-20 chars repeated 3+ times)
    55→    for plen in range(3, min(21, len(text) // 3 + 1)):
    56→        pattern = text[:plen]
    57→        count = 0
    58→        for i in range(0, len(text) - plen + 1, plen):
    59→            if text[i : i + plen] == pattern:
    60→                count += 1
    61→            else:
    62→                break
    63→        if count >= 3 and count * plen >= len(text) * 0.7:
    64→            return True
    65→
    66→    # Check 3: schema description regurgitation
    67→    if _SCHEMA_LEAK_RE.search(text):
    68→        return True
    69→
    70→    return False
    71→
    72→class RepetitionLoopError(RuntimeError):
    73→    """Raised when a Gemini structured-output response contains repetition artifacts."""
    74→
    75→
    76→def check_model_for_repetition(model: BaseModel) -> list[str]:
    77→    """Recursively inspect all string fields of a Pydantic model for repetition.
    78→
    79→    Returns a list of field paths that contain repetition artifacts,
    80→    e.g. ["mappings[0].value.unit", "mappings[1].value.value"].
    81→    """
    82→    bad_fields: list[str] = []
    83→
    84→    def _check(obj: Any, prefix: str) -> None:
    85→        if isinstance(obj, BaseModel):
    86→            for field_name in obj.model_fields:
    87→                _check(getattr(obj, field_name, None), f"{prefix}.{field_name}")
    88→        elif isinstance(obj, list):
    89→            for i, item in enumerate(obj):
    90→                _check(item, f"{prefix}[{i}]")
    91→        elif isinstance(obj, str) and is_repetition_loop(obj):
    92→            bad_fields.append(prefix)
    93→
    94→    _check(model, "root")
    95→    return bad_fields
    96→
    97→
    98→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
    99→
   100→
   101→def create_structured_llm(
   102→    output_schema: type[T],
   103→) -> Any | None:
   104→    """Create an LLM client with structured output.
   105→
   106→    Tries Gemini first (GOOGLE_API_KEY), falls back to Ollama
   107→    (OLLAMA_BASE_URL) if Gemini is not configured.
   108→
   109→    Args:
   110→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
   111→
   112→    Returns:
   113→        A structured LLM instance, or None if no backend is available.
   114→    """
   115→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   116→    if google_api_key:
   117→        try:
   118→            from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
   119→
   120→            gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   121→            gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   122→                model=gemini_model_name,
   123→                google_api_key=google_api_key,
   124→                max_output_tokens=2048,
   125→            )
   126→            return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
   127→        except Exception as e:
   128→            logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
   129→
   130→    # Fallback to Ollama
   131→    ollama_base_url = os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL")
   132→    if ollama_base_url:
   133→        try:
   134→            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama
   135→
   136→            ollama_model = os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "gemma2:9b")
   137→            logger.info(
   138→                "Using Ollama for structured output: %s/%s",
   139→                ollama_base_url,
   140→                ollama_model,
   141→            )
   142→            ollama = ChatOllama(
   143→                base_url=ollama_base_url,
   144→                model=ollama_model,
   145→                format="json",
   146→                temperature=0.1,
   147→            )
   148→            return ollama.with_structured_output(output_schema)
   149→        except Exception as e:
   150→            logger.warning("Failed to create Ollama client: %s", e)
   151→
   152→    logger.warning("No LLM backend available — skipping structured output call")
   153→    return None
   154→
   155→
   156→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
   157→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
   158→
   159→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
   160→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
   161→
   162→    Args:
   163→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
   164→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
   165→
   166→    Returns:
   167→        Validated model instance.
   168→    """
   169→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   170→        return model.model_validate(result)
   171→    return result  # type: ignore[no-any-return]
   172→
```

     1→"""Field mapping generation tool for grounded entities.
     2→
     3→Per user decision: "Generate suggested field_mappings during grounding (ground node)"
     4→Per CONTEXT.md: "Criteria should be decomposed per AutoCriteria pattern: separate
     5→Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time"
     6→Per user decision: "MedGemma and Gemini collaborate: Gemini uses MedGemma as
     7→medical expert"
     8→
     9→Uses Gemini to generate suggested field mappings for the grounded entity +
    10→criterion text. These are best-effort suggestions — reviewer can edit in UI.
    11→"""
    12→
    13→from __future__ import annotations
    14→
    15→import logging
    16→from typing import Any, Literal
    17→
    18→from pydantic import BaseModel, Field, field_validator, model_validator
    19→
    20→from protocol_processor.prompts import render_template
    21→from protocol_processor.schemas.grounding import EntityGroundingResult
    22→from protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (
    23→    RepetitionLoopError,
    24→    check_model_for_repetition,
    25→    create_structured_llm,
    26→    is_repetition_loop,
    27→    parse_structured_output,
    28→)
    29→
    30→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    31→
    32→# Mapping from legacy/LLM relation operators to the frontend's RelationOperator set
    33→_RELATION_MAP: dict[str, str] = {
    34→    "has": "contains",
    35→    "is": "=",
    36→    "not": "not_contains",
    37→    "==": "=",
    38→    "range": "within",
    39→}
    40→
    41→
    42→def _normalize_relation(rel: str) -> str:
    43→    """Normalize a relation operator to the frontend's accepted set.
    44→
    45→    Maps legacy operators (has, is, not, ==, range) to the standard set:
    46→    =, !=, >, >=, <, <=, within, not_in_last, contains, not_contains.
    47→    """
    48→    return _RELATION_MAP.get(rel, rel)
    49→
    50→
    51→class FieldMappingValue(BaseModel):
    52→    """Typed value object for field mappings with type discriminator.
    53→
    54→    Supports three value shapes:
    55→    - standard: single value + unit (e.g. HbA1c < 7%)
    56→    - range: min/max + unit (e.g. Age 18-65 years)
    57→    - temporal: duration + unit (e.g. within 6 months)
    58→    """
    59→
    60→    type: Literal["standard", "range", "temporal"] = Field(
    61→        description="Value type discriminator"
    62→    )
    63→    value: str | None = Field(
    64→        default=None, description="Value for standard type (e.g. '7')"
    65→    )
    66→    unit: str | None = Field(
    67→        default=None, description="Unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'months')"
    68→    )
    69→    min: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Minimum value for range type")
    70→    max: str | None = Field(default=None, description="Maximum value for range type")
    71→    duration: str | None = Field(
    72→        default=None, description="Duration value for temporal type"
    73→    )
    74→
    75→    @model_validator(mode="after")
    76→    def sanitize_repetition_artifacts(self) -> "FieldMappingValue":
    77→        """Guard against LLM repetition loops producing absurdly long or repetitive values."""
    78→        _max = 200
    79→        for attr in ("value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"):
    80→            val = getattr(self, attr)
    81→            if not isinstance(val, str):
    82→                continue
    83→            # Null out values that are repetition artifacts
    84→            if is_repetition_loop(val):
    85→                setattr(self, attr, None)
    86→            elif len(val) > _max:
    87→                setattr(self, attr, val[:_max])
    88→        return self
    89→
    90→
    91→# The set of valid relation operators accepted by the frontend
    92→RelationOperator = Literal[
    93→    "=", "!=", ">", ">=", "<", "<=", "within", "not_in_last", "contains", "not_contains"
    94→]
    95→
    96→
    97→class FieldMappingItem(BaseModel):
    98→    """A single AutoCriteria field mapping decomposition.
    99→
   100→    Per the AutoCriteria pattern, each criterion is decomposed into
   101→    separate Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, and Time components.
   102→    """
   103→
   104→    entity: str = Field(description="The medical entity name (e.g. 'HbA1c')")
   105→    relation: RelationOperator = Field(
   106→        description="The logical operator/relation (e.g. '<', '>', '=', 'contains')"
   107→    )
   108→    value: FieldMappingValue = Field(
   109→        description="Typed value object with type discriminator"
   110→    )
   111→    unit: str | None = Field(
   112→        default=None,
   113→        description="Optional unit of measurement (e.g. '%', 'mg/dL', 'years')",
   114→    )
   115→    value_concept_id: str | None = Field(
   116→        default=None,
   117→        description="OMOP concept ID for categorical values",
   118→    )
   119→    value_concept_system: str | None = Field(
   120→        default=None,
   121→        description="Terminology system for value_concept_id (e.g. 'SNOMED', 'OMOP')",
   122→    )
   123→
   124→    @field_validator("relation", mode="before")
   125→    @classmethod
   126→    def normalize_relation(cls, v: str) -> str:
   127→        """Normalize LLM-generated relation operators before Literal validation."""
   128→        if isinstance(v, str):
   129→            return _normalize_relation(v)
   130→        return v
   131→
   132→
   133→class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
   134→    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""
   135→
   136→    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
   137→        default_factory=list,
   138→        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
   139→    )
   140→
   141→
   142→async def generate_field_mappings(
   143→    entity: EntityGroundingResult,
   144→    criterion_text: str,
   145→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   146→    """Generate suggested field mappings for a grounded entity.
   147→
   148→    Uses Gemini to decompose the criterion text into AutoCriteria field mappings:
   149→    Entity, Operator, Value, Unit, Time components for each discrete condition.
   150→
   151→    This is a best-effort suggestion — reviewer can edit in the UI. Errors
   152→    are logged and an empty list returned (not propagated as failures).
   153→
   154→    Args:
   155→        entity: Grounded EntityGroundingResult with code and preferred term.
   156→        criterion_text: Full criterion text for context.
   157→
   158→    Returns:
   159→        List of field mapping dicts with keys: entity, relation, value,
   160→        entity_code, entity_system, omop_concept_id, entity_type.
   161→        Empty list if generation fails.
   162→    """
   163→    if not criterion_text:
   164→        return []
   165→
   166→    structured_llm = create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)
   167→    if structured_llm is None:
   168→        return []
   169→
   170→    # Build a context-rich prompt for field mapping generation
   171→    grounded_term = entity.preferred_term or entity.entity_text
   172→    code_context = ""
   173→    if entity.selected_code and entity.selected_system:
   174→        system = entity.selected_system.upper()
   175→        code_context = f"(grounded to {system} code: {entity.selected_code})"
   176→
   177→    prompt = render_template(
   178→        "field_mapping.jinja2",
   179→        grounded_term=grounded_term,
   180→        code_context=code_context,
   181→        criterion_text=criterion_text,
   182→        entity_type=entity.entity_type or "Condition",
   183→    )
   184→
   185→    max_attempts = 3
   186→    for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
   187→        try:
   188→            from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   189→
   190→            with llm_span("gemini_field_mapping") as llm:
   191→                llm.set_request(prompt)
   192→                result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   193→                llm.set_response(str(result))
   194→
   195→            response = parse_structured_output(result, FieldMappingResponse)
   196→
   197→            # Check for Gemini repetition loop artifacts in the response
   198→            bad_fields = check_model_for_repetition(response)
   199→            if bad_fields:
   200→                if attempt < max_attempts:
   201→                    logger.warning(
   202→                        "Repetition loop detected in field mapping for '%s' "
   203→                        "(attempt %d/%d, fields: %s) — retrying",
   204→                        entity.entity_text[:50],
   205→                        attempt,
   206→                        max_attempts,
   207→                        bad_fields,
   208→                    )
   209→                    continue
   210→                logger.warning(
   211→                    "Repetition loop persisted after %d attempts for '%s' "
   212→                    "(fields: %s) — returning sanitized result",
   213→                    max_attempts,
   214→                    entity.entity_text[:50],
   215→                    bad_fields,
   216→                )
   217→
   218→            mappings = [
   219→                {
   220→                    "entity": m.entity,
   221→                    "relation": m.relation,
   222→                    "value": m.value.model_dump(exclude_none=True),
   223→                    "entity_code": entity.selected_code,
   224→                    "entity_system": entity.selected_system,
   225→                    "omop_concept_id": entity.omop_concept_id,
   226→                    "entity_type": entity.entity_type,
   227→                    "value_concept_id": m.value_concept_id,
   228→                    "value_concept_system": m.value_concept_system,
   229→                }
   230→                for m in response.mappings
   231→            ]
   232→
   233→            logger.info(
   234→                "Generated %d field mapping(s) for entity '%s'",
   235→                len(mappings),
   236→                entity.entity_text[:50],
   237→            )
   238→            return mappings
   239→
   240→        except Exception as e:
   241→            logger.warning(
   242→                "Field mapping generation failed for entity '%s' "
   243→                "(attempt %d/%d): %s",
   244→                entity.entity_text[:50],
   245→                attempt,
   246→                max_attempts,
   247→                e,
   248→                exc_info=True,
   249→            )
   250→            if attempt == max_attempts:
   251→                break
   252→
   253→    # Best-effort: return empty list on failure
   254→    return []

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/tests/e2e/test_repetition_detection.py",
  "content": "\"\"\"Quick validation of repetition loop detection and sanitization.\"\"\"\n\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.gemini_utils import (\n    is_repetition_loop,\n    check_model_for_repetition,\n)\nfrom protocol_processor.tools.field_mapper import FieldMappingValue, FieldMappingResponse, FieldMappingItem\n\n\ndef test_detection():\n    \"\"\"Test is_repetition_loop() against known patterns.\"\"\"\n    test_cases = [\n        (\"2202202020202020202020202020202020\", True, \"repeating digits\"),\n        (\n            \"8598739459392231e-3158739459392231e-3158739459392231e-315\",\n            True,\n            \"repeating sci notation\",\n        ),\n        (\"2Unit of measurement (e.g. years)\", True, \"schema leak\"),\n        (\"Value for standard type\", True, \"schema leak 2\"),\n        (\"Duration value for temporal type\", True, \"schema leak 3\"),\n        (\"True\", False, \"normal boolean\"),\n        (\"7\", False, \"normal number\"),\n        (\"%\", False, \"normal unit\"),\n        (\"mg/dL\", False, \"normal unit 2\"),\n        (\"HbA1c\", False, \"normal entity\"),\n        (\"44\", False, \"normal value\"),\n    ]\n\n    print(\"=== is_repetition_loop() unit tests ===\")\n    all_pass = True\n    for text, expected, label in test_cases:\n        result = is_repetition_loop(text)\n        status = \"OK\" if result == expected else \"FAIL\"\n        if status == \"FAIL\":\n            all_pass = False\n        print(f\"  {status}: {label:30s} | expected={expected}, got={result}\")\n\n    print()\n    return all_pass\n\n\ndef test_sanitization():\n    \"\"\"Test FieldMappingValue validator nulls out repetitive values.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=== FieldMappingValue sanitization ===\")\n\n    # Repetitive value + schema-leak unit should both be nulled\n    val = FieldMappingValue(\n        type=\"standard\",\n        value=\"2202202020202020202020202020202020\",\n        unit=\"Unit of measurement (e.g. years)\",\n    )\n    print(f\"  value after sanitization: {val.value!r} (expected None)\")\n    print(f\"  unit after sanitization:  {val.unit!r} (expected None)\")\n    assert val.value is None, f\"Expected None, got {val.value!r}\"\n    assert val.unit is None, f\"Expected None, got {val.unit!r}\"\n\n    # Normal values should be preserved\n    val2 = FieldMappingValue(type=\"standard\", value=\"7\", unit=\"%\")\n    print(f\"  normal value preserved:   {val2.value!r} (expected '7')\")\n    print(f\"  normal unit preserved:    {val2.unit!r} (expected '%')\")\n    assert val2.value == \"7\"\n    assert val2.unit == \"%\"\n\n    print()\n    return True\n\n\ndef test_model_check():\n    \"\"\"Test check_model_for_repetition() on a full response.\"\"\"\n    print(\"=== check_model_for_repetition() ===\")\n\n    # Response with one clean mapping and one repetitive mapping\n    response = FieldMappingResponse(\n        mappings=[\n            FieldMappingItem(\n                entity=\"HbA1c\",\n                relation=\"<\",\n                value=FieldMappingValue(type=\"standard\", value=\"7\", unit=\"%\"),\n            ),\n            FieldMappingItem(\n                entity=\"Postmenopause\",\n                relation=\"=\",\n                value=FieldMappingValue(\n                    type=\"standard\",\n                    value=\"2202202020202020202020202020202020\",\n                    unit=None,\n                ),\n            ),\n        ]\n    )\n\n    # Note: the validator will have already nulled out the repetitive value\n    # during Pydantic construction. So check_model_for_repetition should\n    # find nothing (since the validator cleaned it). Let's verify.\n    bad = check_model_for_repetition(response)\n    print(f\"  Bad fields after Pydantic sanitization: {bad}\")\n    print(f\"  (expected empty — validator already cleaned)\")\n    print(f\"  Mapping[1] value.value = {response.mappings[1].value.value!r}\")\n\n    # Now test with a raw dict that bypasses the validator\n    # (simulating what check_model_for_repetition would see BEFORE validation)\n    from pydantic import BaseModel, Field\n\n    class RawValue(BaseModel):\n        type: str = \"standard\"\n        value: str | None = None\n\n    class RawMapping(BaseModel):\n        entity: str = \"\"\n        mappings: list[RawValue] = Field(default_factory=list)\n\n    raw = RawMapping(\n        mappings=[\n            RawValue(value=\"ok\"),\n            RawValue(value=\"220220220220220220220220220220220220\"),\n        ]\n    )\n    bad2 = check_model_for_repetition(raw)\n    print(f\"  Raw model bad fields: {bad2}\")\n    assert len(bad2) == 1, f\"Expected 1 bad field, got {bad2}\"\n\n    print()\n    return True\n\n\nif __name__ == \"__main__\":\n    p1 = test_detection()\n    p2 = test_sanitization()\n    p3 = test_model_check()\n\n    if p1 and p2 and p3:\n        print(\"ALL TESTS PASSED\")\n    else:\n        print(\"SOME TESTS FAILED\")\n        exit(1)\n"
}

> AGENT

One failure: the repeating scientific notation pattern `"8598739459392231e-315"` repeated 3 times isn't caught because the repeating unit is 21 chars but the loop only checks up to 20. Also, in production the repetition can start mid-string (e.g. `"2 statistics.8598739459392231e-315..."`) which breaks the position-0 assumption. Let me fix the detector.

> TOOL

tool_use Edit
id: toolu_01WCxGDDoRVufF4xdNRWKqer
```json
{
  "replace_all": false,
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/gemini_utils.py",
  "old_string": "    # Check 2: short repeating substring (3-20 chars repeated 3+ times)\n    for plen in range(3, min(21, len(text) // 3 + 1)):\n        pattern = text[:plen]\n        count = 0\n        for i in range(0, len(text) - plen + 1, plen):\n            if text[i : i + plen] == pattern:\n                count += 1\n            else:\n                break\n        if count >= 3 and count * plen >= len(text) * 0.7:\n            return True",
  "new_string": "    # Check 2: repeating substring (3-30 chars repeated 3+ times)\n    # Tries from position 0 first, then from later offsets to catch\n    # repetition that starts after a short prefix.\n    max_plen = min(31, len(text) // 3 + 1)\n    for start in range(0, min(20, len(text) // 2)):\n        remaining = text[start:]\n        if len(remaining) < 9:\n            break\n        for plen in range(3, min(max_plen, len(remaining) // 3 + 1)):\n            pattern = remaining[:plen]\n            count = 0\n            for i in range(0, len(remaining) - plen + 1, plen):\n                if remaining[i : i + plen] == pattern:\n                    count += 1\n                else:\n                    break\n            if count >= 3 and count * plen >= len(remaining) * 0.7:\n                return True"
}

Medically acceptable methods of contraception '\n    'include: abstinence, oral contraceptive (with barrier contraceptive), '\n    'hormonal method, IUD, or barrier contraceptive.'\n)\n\nentities = [\n    EntityGroundingResult(\n        entity_text='Oral Contraceptive',\n        entity_type='Medication',\n        selected_code='C0009905',\n        selected_system='UMLS',\n        preferred_term='Oral Contraceptives',\n        confidence=0.9,\n    ),\n    EntityGroundingResult(\n        entity_text='Postmenopause',\n        entity_type='Condition',\n        selected_code='C0206159',\n        selected_system='UMLS',\n        preferred_term='Postmenopause',\n        confidence=0.9,\n    ),\n    EntityGroundingResult(\n        entity_text='Surgical sterilization',\n        entity_type='Procedure',\n        selected_code='C0585024',\n        selected_system='UMLS',\n        preferred_term='Surgical sterilization',\n        confidence=0.9,\n    ),\n]\n\nasync def main():\n    print('='*60)\n    print('REPRODUCTION TEST: Compound criterion field mapping')\n    print('='*60)\n    for ent in entities:\n        print(f'\\nEntity: {ent.entity_text} ({ent.entity_type})')\n        mappings = await generate_field_mappings(ent, COMPOUND_CRITERION)\n        for m in mappings:\n            v = m['value']\n            print(f'  relation={m[\\\"relation\\\"]}  value={v}')\n            # Check for any suspiciously long strings\n            for k, val in v.items():\n                if isinstance(val, str) and len(val) > 30:\n                    print(f'  WARNING: {k} is suspiciously long: {val[:50]}...')\n        if not mappings:\n            print('  (no mappings returned)')\n    print()\n    print('Done.')\n\nasyncio.run(main())\n\" 2>&1 | grep -v \"^protocol_processor\\.\" | grep -v \"^httpx \" | grep -v \"^google\\.\" | grep -v \"^langchain\"",
  "description": "Live reproduction test with compound criterion against Gemini API",
  "timeout": 120000
}

## MLFlow Traces Storage and Repetition Loop Analysis

Based on my exploration of your project, here's a complete guide to MLFlow traces and how to query for repetition loops:

### 1. **MLFlow Storage Locations**

**Database:**
- Location: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/.mlflow/mlflow.db`
- Type: SQLite database
- Contains: 3,783 traces across all experiments

**Trace Artifacts:**
- Location: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/.mlflow/artifacts/{experiment_id}/traces/{trace_id}/artifacts/traces.json`
- Each trace has a JSON file containing span details with inputs/outputs

**Configuration:**
- File: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/.env`
- Key setting: `MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI=http://localhost:5001`

### 2. **MLFlow Database Schema for Traces**

Key tables in `mlflow.db`:

```
trace_info (trace_id/request_id, experiment_id, status, timestamp_ms)
trace_tags (key, value, request_id) - for grouping/filtering traces
spans (trace_id, span_id, parent_span_id, name, status, start_time_unix_nano, end_time_unix_nano, content)
```

### 3. **Tracing Implementation Details**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tracing.py`

**Key Functions:**
- `pipeline_span(name, span_type="CHAIN", protocol_id)` - Creates independent MLflow traces for pipeline nodes
- `llm_span(name, model_name)` - Creates child spans for LLM calls
- `set_pipeline_run_id(run_id)` - Tags traces with a shared run_id
- Uses context variables to pass run_id across async boundaries

**Important Design Notes:**
- Each node creates a **separate, independent trace** (not nested)
- Traces are tagged with: `protocol_id`, `run_id`, and `node` name
- `langchain.autolog()` is DISABLED to allow real-time trace flushing

### 4. **Trigger Configuration**

**Location:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/trigger.py`

**Key Configuration:**
- `_ensure_mlflow()` - Sets up MLflow tracking (called at startup)
- Experiment naming: `protocol-processing-YYYYMMDD` (one per day)
- Orphan cleanup: Closes stale IN_PROGRESS traces older than 1 hour
- Thread-safe: MLflow setup happens inside `asyncio.run()` context

### 5. **Querying for Repetition Loops**

**SQL Query to Find Spans with High Repetition (4+ occurrences):**

```sql
SELECT 
    trace_id, 
    name, 
    COUNT(*) as repetition_count,
    MIN(start_time_unix_nano) as first_start,
    MAX(end_time_unix_nano) as last_end,
    (MAX(end_time_unix_nano) - MIN(start_time_unix_nano)) / 1000000000.0 as duration_seconds
FROM spans 
GROUP BY trace_id, name 
HAVING COUNT(*) > 3 
ORDER BY repetition_count DESC
LIMIT 50;
```

**Real-World Results from Your Database:**

Top repetition loops detected:
- `tr-1f4a3a2a0b5555cc6f50147e7f5ed62f`: `medgemma_evaluate` (11 times), `medgemma_reasoning` (9 times)
- `tr-e9ce05e72e79ff9e8e138d8e36237d21`: `gemini_structure_decision` (11 times), `medgemma_evaluate` (11 times), `gemini_field_mapping` (9 times)
- `tr-0c2601fc7ef4b2b142b257ce772fab0b`: Multiple LLM calls repeating 6-8 times

**Python Query Example:**

```python
import os
import sqlite3

def find_repetition_loops(min_repetitions: int = 4, limit: int = 50):
    """Find traces with repeated span names (potential loops)."""
    db_path = "/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/.mlflow/mlflow.db"
    conn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)
    conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
    cursor = conn.cursor()
    
    cursor.execute("""
        SELECT 
            trace_id, 
            name, 
            COUNT(*) as repetition_count,
            MIN(start_time_unix_nano) as first_start,
            MAX(end_time_unix_nano) as last_end,
            ROUND((MAX(end_time_unix_nano) - MIN(start_time_unix_nano)) / 1000000000.0, 2) as duration_seconds,
            GROUP_CONCAT(status) as statuses
        FROM spans 
        GROUP BY trace_id, name 
        HAVING COUNT(*) > ?
        ORDER BY repetition_count DESC
        LIMIT ?
    """, (min_repetitions - 1, limit))
    
    results = []
    for row in cursor.fetchall():
        results.append({
            'trace_id': row['trace_id'],
            'span_name': row['name'],
            'repetition_count': row['repetition_count'],
            'duration_seconds': row['duration_seconds'],
            'statuses': row['statuses'].split(',')
        })
    
    conn.close()
    return results
```

### 6. **Accessing Trace Artifacts**

**Via MLFlow Client:**

```python
import mlflow
import json

mlflow.set_tracking_uri(os.getenv("MLFLOW_TRACKING_URI"))
client = mlflow.MlflowClient()

# Search traces by protocol_id
traces = client.search_traces(
    filter_string="tags.protocol_id = 'your-protocol-id'",
    max_results=20
)

# Access span details
for trace in traces:
    if trace.data and trace.data.spans:
        for span in trace.data.spans:
            print(f"Span: {span.name}")
            print(f"  Status: {span.status}")
            print(f"  Duration: {(span.end_time_unix_nano - span.start_time_unix_nano) / 1e9:.2f}s")
            # Inputs/outputs stored in span.attributes
```

**Via Direct File Access:**

```python
import json
from pathlib import Path

artifact_path = Path("/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/.mlflow/artifacts")
# Browse structure: artifact_path / {experiment_id} / traces / {trace_id} / artifacts / traces.json

# Each traces.json contains span details with inputs/outputs
with open(traces_json_path) as f:
    data = json.load(f)
    for span in data.get("spans", []):
        print(f"Span: {span['name']}, Status: {span['status']}")
```

### 7. **Related Utility Scripts**

**Cleanup Utility:**
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/scripts/clear_mlflow_traces.py` - Delete all traces
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/scripts/verify_mlflow_tracing.py` - Test trace configuration

**Analysis Scripts:**
- `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/scripts/harvest_failures.py` - Queries failures and fetches traces via `client.search_traces(filter_string=f"tags.protocol_id = '{pid}'")`

### 8. **Key Insights for Repetition Loop Detection**

1. **Loop Indicators:** When `COUNT(*) > 3` for the same `(trace_id, name)` pair
2. **Duration Analysis:** Compare `(end_time - start_time) / 1e9` to identify slow loops
3. **Status Patterns:** Check if repeated spans have ERROR status (indicates retry loops)
4. **Hierarchy:** Use `parent_span_id` to understand nested retry loops
5. **Timestamp Gaps:** Analyze time between sequential spans with same name to find sleep/wait periods

Your database currently shows 30+ traces with high repetition (5-11 iterations), primarily in LLM evaluation and reasoning spans, suggesting potential retry loops or iterative refinement patterns.

## Complete Call Chain Trace: Field Mapping Generation to Gemini API

2. Calls `create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)` (line 165)

### **Step 1: LLM Factory - `create_structured_llm()`**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/gemini_utils.py`

**Lines 118-170**

```python
def create_structured_llm(
    output_schema: type[T],
) -> Any | None:
```

**Exact Configuration:**

```python
gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    model=gemini_model_name,
    google_api_key=google_api_key,
    max_output_tokens=2048,
)
return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
```

**Key Details:**
- **Model:** `gemini-2.5-flash` (default, configurable via `GEMINI_MODEL_NAME` env var)
- **API:** Google Generative AI (via `langchain_google_genai`)
- **Temperature:** NOT set (uses Gemini's default of 0.7)
- **max_output_tokens:** **2048** — this is the critical parameter
- **Structured Output Wrapper:** LangChain's `with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)`

---

### **Step 2: Structured Output Wrapper - LangChain's `with_structured_output()`**

**Library:** `langchain_google_genai>=4.2.0` (from pyproject.toml line 13)

The `with_structured_output()` method:
1. Takes a Pydantic model class (`FieldMappingResponse`)
2. Configures Gemini to return JSON matching that schema
3. Wraps the response parsing
4. Returns an async-callable object with `.ainvoke()` method

**Important:** This method uses Google's native JSON response schema support (JSON mode in Gemini API), which enforces strict schema validation but can trigger Gemini's known repetition loop bug.

---

### **Step 3: Prompt Template**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/prompts/field_mapping.jinja2`

**Lines 1-28**

The prompt:
- Sets role as "Clinical trial protocol analyst"
- Provides entity context (medical entity name, code, criterion text)
- Specifies rules for decomposition into Entity-Relation-Value-Unit
- Contains 21 rules including:
  - **CRITICAL FIX (line 9):** "Generate EXACTLY ONE mapping for the medical entity above. Do NOT map other entities"
  - Boolean normalization rules
  - Value type handling (standard, range, temporal)
  - Relation operator extraction
  - Scope isolation for compound criteria

---

### **Step 4: LLM Invocation - `.ainvoke()` Call**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

**Lines 189-192**

```python
with llm_span("gemini_field_mapping") as llm:
    llm.set_request(prompt)
    result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
    llm.set_response(str(result))
```

**What Actually Happens:**

1. LangChain's wrapped `.ainvoke()` sends HTTP request to Gemini API
2. Request includes:
   - The prompt text
   - JSON schema derived from `FieldMappingResponse` Pydantic model
   - `max_output_tokens=2048`
   - Default temperature (0.7)
3. Gemini returns JSON response (or repetition artifact)

---

### **Step 5: Repetition Detection & Retry Logic**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/gemini_utils.py`

**Lines 46-112 (Detection) + field_mapper.py lines 196-207 (Retry)**

**Detection Function:** `is_repetition_loop(text)`
- Checks for low character diversity (< 0.08 unique ratio for strings > 30 chars)
- Detects repeating substrings (3-30 chars repeated 3+ times)
- Detects schema description regurgitation via regex

**Retry Mechanism in field_mapper:**
```python
max_attempts = 3
for attempt in range(1, max_attempts + 1):
    # ... invoke ...
    bad_fields = check_model_for_repetition(response)
    if bad_fields:
        if attempt < max_attempts:
            logger.warning("Repetition loop detected... retrying")
            continue
        logger.warning("Repetition loop persisted after %d attempts", max_attempts)
```

def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
    if isinstance(result, dict):
        return model.model_validate(result)
    return result  # already parsed by LangChain
```

**Step 7: Value Sanitization**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

**Lines 74-87 (FieldMappingValue validator)**

```python
@model_validator(mode="after")
def sanitize_repetition_artifacts(self) -> "FieldMappingValue":
    """Guard against Gemini repetition loops in values."""
    for attr in ("value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"):
        val = getattr(self, attr)
        if is_repetition_loop(val):
            setattr(self, attr, None)
        elif len(val) > 200:
            setattr(self, attr, val[:_max])
    return self
```

This validator runs AFTER Pydantic model creation, catching any repetition artifacts that slipped through.

### **Step 8: Output Construction**

**File:** `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py`

**Lines 217-237**

Returns a list of field mapping dicts with keys:
- `entity`: entity name
- `relation`: normalized operator
- `value`: typed value dict (with `type`, `value`/`min`/`max`/`duration`, `unit`)
- `entity_code`: grounding code
- `entity_system`: grounding system (UMLS, SNOMED, etc.)
- `omop_concept_id`: OMOP concept
- `entity_type`: Condition, Lab_Value, etc.

---

## **Key Findings: Root Cause of Repetition**

### **The Repetition Loop Bug is a Known Gemini Issue**

**Source:** Comments in gemini_utils.py (lines 10-14)
- GitHub issues:
  - https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449
  - https://github.com/googleapis/python-genai/issues/1039

**When it happens:**
1. Gemini's JSON schema mode enters a token repetition loop
2. Model outputs patterns like `"220220220220..."` or `"8598739459392231e-315"`
3. Continues until `max_output_tokens=2048` is exhausted
4. Returns malformed JSON

### **Why `max_output_tokens=2048` is Critical**

From commit `4e3c179` (Feb 25, 2026):
> "Without a cap, Gemini can enter repetition loops producing garbage strings or timeout, causing silent failures via broad except handlers."

**The fix:** Set `max_output_tokens=2048` on ALL Gemini calls
- Prevents infinite repetition loops (repetition stops at 2048 tokens)
- Makes failures detectable (can catch repetition patterns)
- Enables retry logic (3 attempts to get valid response)

### **Why `with_structured_output()` Triggers This**

LangChain's `with_structured_output()` uses Gemini's JSON schema mode:
1. Sends schema as part of the request
2. Instructs Gemini: "respond only with valid JSON matching this schema"
3. Gemini's JSON mode has the repetition bug (not related to LangChain itself)
4. When Gemini struggles to fit output to schema, it can enter repetition loop

**NOT a LangChain bug** — it's a Gemini API behavior under schema constraints.

---

## **MedGemma's Role (Important Clarification)**

**MedGemma is NOT involved in field mapping generation.**

The call chain is:
1. **Field Mapper** → Calls `create_structured_llm()` → Returns Gemini-only client
2. **No MedGemma involvement** in structured output generation

**However, MedGemma IS used elsewhere:**
- In `medgemma_decider.py` (lines 117-150): Uses MedGemma for medical reasoning, THEN passes result to Gemini for structuring
- In `entity_decomposer.py` (lines 181+): Fallback when Gemini fails

---

## **Complete Parameter Summary**

### **For `generate_field_mappings()` → Gemini API Request:**

| Parameter | Value | Source |
|-----------|-------|--------|
| **Model** | `gemini-2.5-flash` | env `GEMINI_MODEL_NAME` or hardcoded default |
| **API** | Google Generative AI (REST) | langchain_google_genai library |
| **max_output_tokens** | 2048 | gemini_utils.py line 141 |
| **temperature** | 0.7 (Gemini default) | NOT explicitly set |
| **response_format** | JSON schema from Pydantic | `.with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)` |
| **Structured Output** | YES - LangChain's `with_structured_output()` | gemini_utils.py line 143 |
| **Schema** | `FieldMappingResponse` (Pydantic model) | field_mapper.py lines 132-138 |

---

## **Why Repetition is Being Caught and Retried**

1. **Detection:** `is_repetition_loop()` identifies patterns in response strings
2. **Validation:** Pydantic validator `sanitize_repetition_artifacts()` nulls out bad fields
3. **Retry:** `generate_field_mappings()` implements 3-attempt loop with detection
4. **Fallback:** Returns empty list if all 3 attempts fail (graceful degradation)

This is a **defensive architecture** that acknowledges Gemini's known bug and handles it robustly.

{
  "command": "set -a && source .env && set +a && PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1 uv run python -c \"\nimport sqlite3\nimport json\nimport re\n\ndb_path = '.mlflow/mlflow.db'\nconn = sqlite3.connect(db_path)\nconn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row\ncursor = conn.cursor()\n\n# Get ALL spans and search for repetition in any of them\ncursor.execute(\\\"\\\"\\\"\n    SELECT trace_id, span_id, name, type, status, content,\n           (end_time_unix_nano - start_time_unix_nano) / 1000000 as duration_ms\n    FROM spans\n    WHERE name LIKE '%gemini%' OR name LIKE '%medgemma%'\n    ORDER BY start_time_unix_nano DESC\n\\\"\\\"\\\")\n\nprint('=== ALL LLM spans with repetition artifacts ===')\nprint()\nprint(f'{\\\"Span Name\\\":45s} {\\\"Duration\\\":>10s} {\\\"Artifact Type\\\":20s} Artifact Preview')\nprint('-' * 130)\n\nartifact_by_span = {}\nfor row in cursor.fetchall():\n    content = json.loads(row['content']) if row['content'] else {}\n    attrs = content.get('attributes', {})\n    outputs_raw = str(attrs.get('mlflow.spanOutputs', ''))\n    \n    suspects = []\n    \n    # Repeating digits\n    digit_runs = re.findall(r'\\d{20,}', outputs_raw)\n    if digit_runs:\n        suspects.append(('repeating_digits', digit_runs[0][:50]))\n    \n    # Schema leaks\n    if re.search(r'(?:Unit of measurement|Value for standard|Duration value for|measurement unit|provided schema|In the context of)', outputs_raw, re.I):\n        match = re.search(r'(?:Unit of measurement|Value for standard|Duration value for|measurement unit|provided schema|In the context of)[^\\\"]{0,60}', outputs_raw, re.I)\n        suspects.append(('schema_leak', match.group(0)[:50] if match else ''))\n    \n    # Sci notation repeats\n    if re.search(r'[0-9.]+e[+-]?\\d+(?:[0-9.]+e[+-]?\\d+)+', outputs_raw):\n        match = re.search(r'[0-9.]+e[+-]?\\d+(?:[0-9.]+e[+-]?\\d+)+', outputs_raw)\n        suspects.append(('sci_notation', match.group(0)[:50] if match else ''))\n    \n    if suspects:\n        for artifact_type, preview in suspects:\n            print(f'{row[\\\"name\\\"]:45s} {row[\\\"duration_ms\\\"]:>8d}ms {artifact_type:20s} {preview}')\n            artifact_by_span.setdefault(row['name'], []).append(artifact_type)\n\nprint()\nprint('=== Summary by Span Type ===')\nfor span_name, artifacts in sorted(artifact_by_span.items()):\n    from collections import Counter\n    counts = Counter(artifacts)\n    print(f'  {span_name}: {dict(counts)}')\n\nconn.close()\n\"\n",
  "description": "Categorize ALL repetition artifacts by span type across entire MLFlow DB"
}

{

start_time_unix_nano) / 1000000 as duration_ms,\n           datetime(start_time_unix_nano / 1000000000, 'unixepoch', 'localtime') as start_time\n    FROM spans\n    WHERE name = 'gemini_field_mapping'\n    ORDER BY start_time_unix_nano DESC\n\\\"\\\"\\\")\n\nimport re\nprint()\nprint('=== gemini_field_mapping: ACTUAL repetition artifacts only ===')\nprint()\nfor row in cursor.fetchall():\n    content = json.loads(row['content']) if row['content'] else {}\n    attrs = content.get('attributes', {})\n    outputs_raw = str(attrs.get('mlflow.spanOutputs', ''))\n    inputs_raw = str(attrs.get('mlflow.spanInputs', ''))\n    \n    # Only look for REAL artifacts: repeating digits, sci notation, \n    # Pydantic field descriptions in value fields\n    digit_runs = re.findall(r'\\d{20,}', outputs_raw)\n    sci_repeats = re.findall(r'[0-9.]+e[+-]?\\d+(?:[0-9.]+e[+-]?\\d+)+', outputs_raw)\n    pydantic_leaks = re.findall(r'(?:Unit of measurement|Value for standard type|Duration value for)', outputs_raw, re.I)\n    \n    if digit_runs or sci_repeats or pydantic_leaks:\n        # Extract entity name from input\n        entity_match = re.search(r'Medical entity: ([^(]+)', inputs_raw)\n        entity_name = entity_match.group(1).strip() if entity_match else '?'\n        \n        artifact = ''\n        if digit_runs:\n            artifact = f'DIGITS: {digit_runs[0][:60]}'\n        elif sci_repeats:\n            artifact = f'SCI: {sci_repeats[0][:60]}'\n        elif pydantic_leaks:\n            artifact = f'PYDANTIC: {pydantic_leaks[0]}'\n        \n        print(f'Time: {row[\\\"start_time\\\"]}  Duration: {row[\\\"duration_ms\\\"]:>8d}ms  Entity: {entity_name[:40]}')\n        print(f'  Artifact: {artifact}')\n        print()\n\nconn.close()\n\"\n",

> AGENT

Now I have all the evidence. Here are my findings:

---

## Root Cause Investigation: Repeating Digits in Production

### Which component produces the artifact?

**100% `gemini_field_mapping`** — the Gemini structured output call via LangChain's `with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)`. 

Out of 547 total `gemini_field_mapping` spans in MLFlow, **10 contain repetition artifacts** (1.8% failure rate).

No other component produces actual repetition artifacts:
- **MedGemma** (`medgemma_evaluate`, `medgemma_reasoning`) — never produces repeating digits. The phrase "in the context of" appearing in my search was a false positive — it's legitimate medical reasoning text.
- **`gemini_structure_decision`** — never produces repeating digits. Same false positive.
- **`gemini_structure_reasoning`** — clean.

### What exactly is Gemini doing?

The call is `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(model="gemini-3-flash-preview", max_output_tokens=2048).with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)`. When Gemini generates JSON matching the `FieldMappingResponse` Pydantic schema, it occasionally enters a **token repetition loop** on string-valued fields, particularly `duration`, `value`, and `unit`.

The artifacts take three forms:
1. **Repeating digits** (8/10 cases): `"222222222222..."`, `"22022022022..."`, `"00000000000..."`
2. **Repeating scientific notation** (1/10): `"8598739459392231e-3158739459392231e-315..."`
3. **Pydantic field description regurgitation** (1/10): `"Unit of measurement (e.g. 'years')"`

### What triggers it?

Every single artifact came from **complex compound criteria** with multiple OR-separated branches:

| Entity | Criterion Type | Duration |
|--------|---------------|----------|
| Postmenopause | Female contraception compound | 11-13s |
| Oral Contraceptive | Female contraception compound | 13-40s |
| Personal history of contraception | Female contraception compound | 12s |
| COVID-19 Virus Disease | COVID symptoms + PCR test compound | 306s |
| Symptoms | COVID PCR + lateral flow test compound | 233s |

The `duration` field of `FieldMappingValue` is the most common victim (8/10 cases).

### Timeline: before vs after `max_output_tokens=2048`

| Period | Duration of artifacts | Impact |
|--------|----------------------|--------|
| Pre-fix (Feb 24-25) | **232-323 seconds** | Model ran for 5+ MINUTES producing garbage |
| Post-fix (Feb 26) | **11-13 seconds** | Capped at 2048 tokens, much faster failure |

The `max_output_tokens=2048` fix (commit `4e3c179`) dramatically reduced the blast radius from minutes of wasted API budget to ~12 seconds, but didn't prevent the artifact from reaching the UI.

### Why Gemini does this

This is a **known, unpatched Gemini bug** ([google-gemini/cookbook#449](https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449), [python-genai#1039](https://github.com/googleapis/python-genai/issues/1039)):

- Gemini's JSON schema mode constrains output to match a Pydantic schema
- When the model is uncertain about what value to produce (ambiguous compound criteria), it can enter a degenerate state where it repeats the same token sequence
- The `frequency_penalty` parameter that could help is **not available** on Gemini 2.5+ models
- No official fix from Google; the recommended workaround is retry + detection

### MedGemma's role

**MedGemma is not involved at all in the failing call chain.** The field mapping generation calls Gemini directly (not via MedGemma). The call chain is:

```
generate_field_mappings()
  → create_structured_llm(FieldMappingResponse)  // creates Gemini client
  → ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output()  // LangChain wrapper
  → Gemini API (JSON schema mode)  // THIS is where the bug lives
```

> DEVELOPER

In my research, I found the following suggestions :Based on the GitHub issue and related discussions, there are several proven solutions for the Gemini 2.0/3.0 Flash structured output repetition problem you're experiencing. [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

## Primary Solution: Add Required Fields

The most reliable fix is to explicitly add the `required` attribute to your JSON schema, listing all field names. This prevents the model from entering repetition loops: [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

```json
{
  "type": "object",
  "properties": {
    "field_1": {"type": "string"},
    "field_2": {"type": "integer"}
  },
  "required": ["field_1", "field_2"]
}
```

If using Pydantic, explicitly mark fields as required using ellipsis (`...`): [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

```python
from pydantic import BaseModel, Field

class YourModel(BaseModel):
    field_1: str = Field(...)
    field_2: int = Field(...)
```

## Additional Mitigation Strategies

1. **Temperature adjustment**: Slightly raising the temperature can help break repetition loops [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

2. **Frequency and presence penalties**: These parameters can reduce repetitive outputs when combined with proper schema definition [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

3. **Input text cleaning**: Removing non-alphanumeric characters before sending to the LLM has resolved issues for some users [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

4. **Model selection**: If the issue persists with Gemini 3.0 Flash, consider using Gemini 2.5 Pro, which appears more stable for structured outputs, or fall back to Gemini 1.5 Flash which handles structured generation more reliably [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

## Unicode-Specific Issue

The repetition with scientific notation and the Pydantic field descriptions you're seeing may be related to unicode escape handling. Gemini 2.0/3.0 Flash models struggle with constrained decoding for unicode escapes, causing loops when trying to generate sequences like `\u00ED` or emoji representations. [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)

The issue has been triaged by the Gemini team but remains open, suggesting it's a known model quality problem rather than a bug in your implementation. [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757) . \n Consider these suggestions and make a plan to fix the problem.
