     1→"""Shared Gemini LLM utilities for protocol processor tools.
     2→
     3→Centralizes the repeated pattern of creating a ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
     4→client with structured output and parsing its response. Used by
     5→structure_builder, ordinal_resolver, and field_mapper.
     6→
     7→Falls back to Ollama when GOOGLE_API_KEY is not set but OLLAMA_BASE_URL
     8→is configured.
     9→
    10→Includes detection for a known Gemini structured-output bug where the
    11→model enters a token repetition loop, producing repeating digits or
    12→regurgitated schema descriptions instead of actual values.  See:
    13→  - https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449
    14→  - https://github.com/googleapis/python-genai/issues/1039
    15→"""
    16→
    17→from __future__ import annotations
    18→
    19→import logging
    20→import os
    21→import re
    22→from typing import Any, TypeVar
    23→
    24→from pydantic import BaseModel
    25→
    26→logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)
    27→
    28→# Pattern that detects schema description regurgitation — the model
    29→# outputs Pydantic field descriptions or chain-of-thought reasoning
    30→# instead of actual values.
    31→_SCHEMA_LEAK_RE = re.compile(
    32→    r"(?:"
    33→    r"Unit of measurement"
    34→    r"|Value for standard type"
    35→    r"|Duration value for"
    36→    r"|measurement unit"
    37→    r"|provided schema"
    38→    r"|In the context of"
    39→    r"|populate the .+ field"
    40→    r"|will populate"
    41→    r")",
    42→    re.IGNORECASE,
    43→)
    44→
    45→
    46→def is_repetition_loop(text: str | None) -> bool:
    47→    """Detect if text contains a Gemini repetition loop.
    48→
    49→    Known Gemini bug: the model enters a token repetition loop during
    50→    structured output generation, producing sequences like
    51→    ``"220220220220220220"`` or ``"8598739459392231e-315"`` repeated
    52→    until ``max_output_tokens`` is exhausted.
    53→
    54→    Returns True if the text is likely a repetition artifact.
    55→    """
    56→    if not text or len(text) < 20:
    57→        return False
    58→
    59→    # Check 1: very low character diversity for the string length
    60→    unique_ratio = len(set(text)) / len(text)
    61→    if unique_ratio < 0.08 and len(text) > 30:
    62→        return True
    63→
    64→    # Check 2: repeating substring (3-30 chars repeated 3+ times)
    65→    # Tries from position 0 first, then from later offsets to catch
    66→    # repetition that starts after a short prefix.
    67→    max_plen = min(31, len(text) // 3 + 1)
    68→    for start in range(0, min(20, len(text) // 2)):
    69→        remaining = text[start:]
    70→        if len(remaining) < 9:
    71→            break
    72→        for plen in range(3, min(max_plen, len(remaining) // 3 + 1)):
    73→            pattern = remaining[:plen]
    74→            count = 0
    75→            for i in range(0, len(remaining) - plen + 1, plen):
    76→                if remaining[i : i + plen] == pattern:
    77→                    count += 1
    78→                else:
    79→                    break
    80→            if count >= 3 and count * plen >= len(remaining) * 0.7:
    81→                return True
    82→
    83→    # Check 3: schema description regurgitation
    84→    if _SCHEMA_LEAK_RE.search(text):
    85→        return True
    86→
    87→    return False
    88→
    89→class RepetitionLoopError(RuntimeError):
    90→    """Raised when a Gemini structured-output response contains repetition artifacts."""
    91→
    92→
    93→def check_model_for_repetition(model: BaseModel) -> list[str]:
    94→    """Recursively inspect all string fields of a Pydantic model for repetition.
    95→
    96→    Returns a list of field paths that contain repetition artifacts,
    97→    e.g. ["mappings[0].value.unit", "mappings[1].value.value"].
    98→    """
    99→    bad_fields: list[str] = []
   100→
   101→    def _check(obj: Any, prefix: str) -> None:
   102→        if isinstance(obj, BaseModel):
   103→            for field_name in obj.model_fields:
   104→                _check(getattr(obj, field_name, None), f"{prefix}.{field_name}")
   105→        elif isinstance(obj, list):
   106→            for i, item in enumerate(obj):
   107→                _check(item, f"{prefix}[{i}]")
   108→        elif isinstance(obj, str) and is_repetition_loop(obj):
   109→            bad_fields.append(prefix)
   110→
   111→    _check(model, "root")
   112→    return bad_fields
   113→
   114→
   115→T = TypeVar("T", bound=BaseModel)
   116→
   117→
   118→def create_structured_llm(
   119→    output_schema: type[T],
   120→) -> Any | None:
   121→    """Create an LLM client with structured output.
   122→
   123→    Tries Gemini first (GOOGLE_API_KEY), falls back to Ollama
   124→    (OLLAMA_BASE_URL) if Gemini is not configured.
   125→
   126→    Args:
   127→        output_schema: Pydantic model class for structured output.
   128→
   129→    Returns:
   130→        A structured LLM instance, or None if no backend is available.
   131→    """
   132→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   133→    if google_api_key:
   134→        try:
   135→            from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI
   136→
   137→            gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   138→            gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   139→                model=gemini_model_name,
   140→                google_api_key=google_api_key,
   141→                max_output_tokens=2048,
   142→            )
   143→            return gemini.with_structured_output(output_schema)
   144→        except Exception as e:
   145→            logger.warning("Failed to create Gemini client: %s", e)
   146→
   147→    # Fallback to Ollama
   148→    ollama_base_url = os.getenv("OLLAMA_BASE_URL")
   149→    if ollama_base_url:
   150→        try:
   151→            from langchain_community.chat_models import ChatOllama
   152→
   153→            ollama_model = os.getenv("OLLAMA_MODEL", "gemma2:9b")
   154→            logger.info(
   155→                "Using Ollama for structured output: %s/%s",
   156→                ollama_base_url,
   157→                ollama_model,
   158→            )
   159→            ollama = ChatOllama(
   160→                base_url=ollama_base_url,
   161→                model=ollama_model,
   162→                format="json",
   163→                temperature=0.1,
   164→            )
   165→            return ollama.with_structured_output(output_schema)
   166→        except Exception as e:
   167→            logger.warning("Failed to create Ollama client: %s", e)
   168→
   169→    logger.warning("No LLM backend available — skipping structured output call")
   170→    return None
   171→
   172→
   173→def parse_structured_output(result: Any, model: type[T]) -> T:
   174→    """Parse a structured LLM output into a Pydantic model.
   175→
   176→    Handles both dict and already-parsed model instances returned
   177→    by LangChain's with_structured_output().
   178→
   179→    Args:
   180→        result: Raw LLM output (dict or model instance).
   181→        model: Pydantic model class to validate against.
   182→
   183→    Returns:
   184→        Validated model instance.
   185→    """
   186→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   187→        return model.model_validate(result)
   188→    return result  # type: ignore[no-any-return]
   189→

> AGENT

There it is. The JSON schema confirms the root cause:

**`FieldMappingValue` has `"required": ["type"]` only.** The fields `value`, `unit`, `min`, `max`, `duration` are all **optional** — they have `default=null` and are NOT in the `required` list. This is exactly the documented trigger for Gemini's repetition loop.

Additionally:
- **No temperature set** for Gemini (defaults to model default ~1.0). Research says lower temperature helps.
- `FieldMappingResponse.mappings` also not in `required` (has `default_factory=list`).

## COMPREHENSIVE ANALYSIS: Pydantic Schemas Passed to `create_structured_llm()`

I found **exactly 3 calls to `create_structured_llm()`** in the codebase. Here's the detailed analysis:

---

### 1. **FieldMappingResponse** *(field_mapper.py, line 165)*

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/tools/field_mapper.py` (lines 132-138)

```python
class FieldMappingResponse(BaseModel):
    """Gemini structured output for field mappings."""

    mappings: list[FieldMappingItem] = Field(
        default_factory=list,
        description="List of AutoCriteria field mapping decompositions",
    )
```

**Analysis:**
- **Total fields**: 1
- **Optional fields**: 1 (has `default_factory=list`)
  - `mappings`: uses `default_factory=list` — will NOT appear in JSON schema `required`
- **JSON schema impact**: The `required` array will be EMPTY (no fields required)
- **Risk level**: **HIGH** — Since `mappings` has a default, Gemini can omit it entirely, returning `{}` instead of `{"mappings": [...]}`. This can cause repetition loops.

**Nested schema** (`FieldMappingItem`, lines 96-130):
- `entity`: required (no default)
- `relation`: required (no default)
- `value`: required (no default)
- `unit`: optional (`default=None`)
- `value_concept_id`: optional (`default=None`)
- `value_concept_system`: optional (`default=None`)

**Nested schema** (`FieldMappingValue`, lines 50-87):
- `type`: required (no default)
- `value`: optional (`default=None`)
- `unit`: optional (`default=None`)
- `min`: optional (`default=None`)
- `max`: optional (`default=None`)
- `duration`: optional (`default=None`)

**Status**: AT RISK for Gemini repetition loop bug

---

### 2. **OrdinalResolutionResponse** *(ordinal_resolver.py, line 47)*

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/ordinal.py` (lines 71-76)

```python
class OrdinalResolutionResponse(BaseModel):
    """Top-level Gemini structured output for ordinal resolution."""

    proposals: list[OrdinalScaleProposal] = Field(
        description="One proposal per candidate entity.",
    )
```

**Analysis:**
- **Total fields**: 1
- **Optional fields**: 0 (no default specified)
- **JSON schema impact**: The `required` array will contain `["proposals"]`
- **Risk level**: **LOW** — The field is required in JSON schema, so Gemini cannot omit it.

**Nested schema** (`OrdinalScaleProposal`, lines 33-68):
- `entity_text`: required
- `is_ordinal_scale`: required
- `confidence`: required
- `scale_name`: optional (`default=None`)
- `entity_aliases`: optional (`default_factory=list`)
- `loinc_code`: optional (`default=None`)
- `unit_concept_id`: optional (has `default=8527`)
- `values`: optional (`default_factory=list`)
- `reasoning`: optional (`default=None`)

**Nested schema** (`OrdinalValueProposal`, lines 13-30):
- `grade`: required
- `snomed_code`: optional (`default=None`)
- `loinc_answer`: optional (`default=None`)
- `description`: optional (`default=None`)

**Status**: SAFE (required field)

---

### 3. **LogicDetectionResponse** *(structure_builder.py, line 127)*

**File**: `/Users/noahdolevelixir/Code/medgemma-hackathon/services/protocol-processor-service/src/protocol_processor/schemas/structure.py` (lines 42-53)

```python
class LogicDetectionResponse(BaseModel):
    """Gemini structured output for logic detection.

    Contains the root of the logic tree and the model's reasoning
    about why it chose this particular logical structure.
    """

    root: LogicNode = Field(description="Root node of the detected logic tree.")
    reasoning: str = Field(
        default="",
        description="Explanation of the detected logical structure.",
    )
```

**Analysis:**
- **Total fields**: 2
- **Optional fields**: 1 (has `default=""`)
  - `reasoning`: uses `default=""` — will NOT appear in JSON schema `required`
- **Required fields**: 1 (`root`)
- **JSON schema impact**: The `required` array will contain `["root"]` (no `reasoning`)
- **Risk level**: **MEDIUM** — The critical `root` field is required, but `reasoning` can be omitted. The default empty string is safe, but if Gemini omits `root`, it will fail.

**Nested schema** (`LogicNode`, lines 17-39):
- `node_type`: required
- `field_mapping_index`: optional (`default=None`)
- `children`: optional (`default=None`)

**Status**: PARTIALLY AT RISK — `root` is required (good), but optional `reasoning` could allow Gemini to skip it

---

## SUMMARY TABLE

| Schema | File | Required Fields in JSON Schema | Optional Fields | Risk Level |
|--------|------|-------------------------------|-----------------|-----------|
| **FieldMappingResponse** | field_mapper.py | NONE (empty!) | `mappings` (default_factory=list) | **HIGH** |
| **OrdinalResolutionResponse** | ordinal.py | `proposals` | None in top level | **LOW** |
| **LogicDetectionResponse** | structure.py | `root` | `reasoning` (default="") | **MEDIUM** |

---

## WHICH SCHEMAS ARE AT RISK FOR GEMINI REPETITION LOOP BUG?

1. **FieldMappingResponse** — **CRITICAL RISK**: All fields have defaults, so the entire schema can be omitted. This is the most dangerous.
2. **LogicDetectionResponse** — **MEDIUM RISK**: While `root` is required, if Gemini enters a repetition loop with the reasoning field, it could cause issues.
3. **OrdinalResolutionResponse** — **LOW RISK**: The top-level `proposals` field is truly required.

**Recommendation**: Fix **FieldMappingResponse** by removing the `default_factory=list` from `mappings` to make it required in the JSON schema.

     1→# Grounding Prompt Variant Experiment
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→We added 2 new grounding test snippets to `test_snippets.json` based on failures discovered in the latest protocol extraction:
     6→1. "Primary indication for TKA is degenerative osteoarthritis of the knee." (entities lost during grounding)
     7→2. Full compound "Female subjects must be surgically sterile; or at least 2 years postmenopausal;..." (decomposition failure)
     8→
     9→We need to run ALL 11 grounding snippets (~24 entities) through 3 prompt variants to find which gets everything right: codes, relations, values, and units. Each variant's calls are traced to MLFlow so we can analyze failure patterns and decide whether a 4th variant is needed.
    10→
    11→## The 3 Variants
    12→
    13→| Variant | MedGemma (code selection) | Field Mapper (relation/value/unit) |
    14→|---------|--------------------------|-------------------------------------|
    15→| **A: Baseline** | Current production prompts | Current production prompts |
    16→| **B: Targeted Fixes** | `exact_match_first` evaluate addition | `operator_rules_v2` field mapper rules |
    17→| **C: Enhanced** | `exact_match_first` + boolean/categorical guidance in system prompt | `operator_rules_v2` + worked examples for boolean entities, score-type values, compound criterion negation |
    18→
    19→- **Variant A** is the control (current code)
    20→- **Variant B** combines the two best-performing fragments from the existing `prompt_variants.py` A/B tests
    21→- **Variant C** extends B with new prompt additions targeting the failure modes in the new test cases
    22→
    23→## Variant C Prompt Additions (New)
    24→
    25→**System prompt addition** (appended to `grounding_system.jinja2`):
    26→```
    27→11. For boolean/presence entities (gender, conditions, procedures), when the
    28→    entity text is a simple medical concept with no numeric qualifier, always
    29→    select the broadest matching concept code. Do not let criterion complexity
    30→    distract from the entity being grounded.
    31→```
    32→
    33→**Field mapper rules addition** (appended to `operator_rules_v2`):
    34→```
    35→- For boolean/presence entities appearing in compound criteria joined by OR/semicolons,
    36→  map ONLY the specific entity to relation='=', value='True'. Ignore other branches.
    37→  Example: criterion "surgically sterile; or postmenopausal; or practicing contraception"
    38→  with entity "Postmenopause" → relation='=', value='True', unit=null
    39→- For ordinal scoring systems (ASA, ECOG, NYHA, etc.) with discrete listed values like
    40→  "1, 2, or 3", use relation='=' with the enumerated values as-is. Do NOT convert to a range.
    41→```
    42→
    43→## Script Architecture
    44→
    45→**New file:** `tests/e2e/run_variant_experiment.py`
    46→
    47→Builds on patterns from `run_prompt_variants.py` and `run_grounding_snippets.py`:
    48→
    49→### Phase 1: Cache (one-time API calls)
    50→- `TerminologyRouter.route_entity()` per entity → candidates cache
    51→- `normalize_unit()` per entity → unit cache (deterministic, no LLM)
    52→
    53→### Phase 2: Run 3 Variants
    54→For each variant (A, B, C):
    55→1. Tag an MLFlow experiment run with `variant={name}`, `experiment_type=prompt_variant`
    56→2. For each entity:
    57→   - `medgemma_decide()` with variant-specific prompts → `selected_code`, `confidence`
    58→   - `generate_field_mappings()` with variant-specific prompts → `relation`, `value`, `unit`
    59→3. Capture per-entity trace data: latency_ms, token counts (from MedGemma + Gemini), error/retry count
    60→
    61→### Phase 3: Score & Compare
    62→Score each variant on 5 dimensions per entity:
    63→- **Code match**: `selected_code == expected`
    64→- **Relation match**: `field_mapping.relation == expected`
    65→- **Value match**: `field_mapping.value == expected`
    66→- **Unit UCUM match**: from `normalize_unit()` cache
    67→- **Unit OMOP match**: from `normalize_unit()` cache
    68→
    69→### Phase 4: MLFlow Trace Analysis
    70→After all 3 variants complete, query the MLFlow trace store to analyze:
    71→
    72→1. **Failure pattern clustering** — group mismatches by entity type (Condition, Procedure, Demographic, Medication, Lab_Value) and failure dimension (code/relation/value). Identifies whether failures are entity-type-specific or prompt-generic.
    73→2. **Confidence vs accuracy** — for each variant, plot confidence scores against actual correctness. Low confidence + correct = conservative model. High confidence + wrong = dangerous hallucination. This tells us if the model "knows what it doesn't know."
    74→3. **Retry effectiveness** — count how many entities required retries per variant and whether retries improved or degraded the outcome. High retry count with no improvement = wasted API budget.
    75→4. **Latency breakdown** — compare median/p95 latency per variant. If variant C is significantly slower without meaningful accuracy gain, B may be preferable.
    76→
    77→Trace data is collected inline during Phase 2 (each `medgemma_decide` and `generate_field_mappings` call already writes MLFlow spans via the existing tracing infrastructure). The script reads back trace metrics from the SQLite store at `.mlflow/mlflow.db` after all variants complete.
    78→
    79→### Phase 5: Decision — Stop or Continue?
    80→The script prints a recommendation based on the results:
    81→
    82→| Outcome | Action |
    83→|---------|--------|
    84→| One variant scores 100% on all dimensions | **Stop.** Declare winner. Print the prompt diff to apply to production. |
    85→| Best variant has ≤2 failures and they're in one entity type | **Stop.** Flag the specific entity type for a targeted 4th variant. Print what the 4th variant should address. |
    86→| Best variant has >2 failures spread across types | **Continue.** Auto-generate a Variant D hypothesis from the failure pattern analysis: which prompt section to change and what the change should be. Save the hypothesis to `prompt_variant_results/variant_d_hypothesis.md`. |
    87→| All variants perform equally (no improvement over baseline) | **Stop.** The prompts aren't the bottleneck — investigate TerminologyRouter candidate quality or model capability instead. |
    88→
    89→## Files to Create/Modify
    90→
    91→| File | Action |
    92→|------|--------|
    93→| `tests/e2e/run_variant_experiment.py` | **Create** — Main experiment script (~350 lines) |
    94→| `tests/e2e/prompt_variants.py` | **Edit** — Add Variant C definitions (ENHANCED_SYSTEM, ENHANCED_FIELD_MAPPER constants) |
    95→
    96→No production code changes. This is a read-only experiment.
    97→
    98→## API Budget
    99→
   100→~24 router calls (cached) + 24×3 MedGemma/Gemini decide + 24×3 field mapper = ~24 + 144 + 72 = ~240 total calls. `asyncio.Semaphore(4)` for rate limiting. Expected runtime: 5-8 minutes.
   101→
   102→## Execution
   103→
   104→```bash
   105→set -a && source .env && set +a && uv run python tests/e2e/run_variant_experiment.py
   106→```
   107→
   108→## Expected Output
   109→
   110→```
   111→═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   112→GROUNDING VARIANT EXPERIMENT — 24 entities × 3 variants
   113→═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   114→
   115→Entity                     Metric     Expected      A:Baseline  B:Targeted  C:Enhanced
   116→──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
   117→Serum creatinine           code       C0201976      OK          OK          OK
   118→                           relation   >             MISS (=)    OK          OK

   120→...
   121→
   122→═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   123→VARIANT SUMMARY
   124→═══════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════════
   125→                    A:Baseline    B:Targeted    C:Enhanced
   126→Code match:         20/24 (83%)   22/24 (92%)   23/24 (96%)
   127→Relation match:     18/24 (75%)   22/24 (92%)   23/24 (96%)
   128→Value match:        20/24 (83%)   22/24 (92%)   23/24 (96%)
   129→Unit UCUM:          5/5 (100%)    5/5 (100%)    5/5 (100%)

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/.claude-elixirtrials/plans/velvety-whistling-catmull.md",
  "content": "# Fix Gemini Structured Output Repetition Loop\n\n## Context\n\nProduction is producing repeating digit artifacts like `\"2202202020202020...\"` in field mapping values. Root cause investigation confirmed:\n\n- **Source**: 100% from `gemini_field_mapping` spans — the `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)` call\n- **NOT** MedGemma, structure_builder, or ordinal_resolver\n- **Frequency**: 10 out of 547 field_mapping spans (1.8%), but concentrated on compound criteria\n- **Impact**: Garbage values displayed in the UI\n\nThe documented root cause (per [google-gemini/cookbook#449](https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449) and [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)) is that Gemini enters token repetition loops when optional fields lack `required` in the JSON schema.\n\n**Current `FieldMappingValue` JSON schema has `\"required\": [\"type\"]` only** — the 5 string fields (`value`, `unit`, `min`, `max`, `duration`) all use `default=None` and are NOT in the required array. This is the exact trigger documented by Google.\n\n## Fix: 3 Layers\n\n### Layer 1: Root Cause Fix — Make All Schema Fields Required\n\nChange all `default=None` and `default_factory=list` fields to use `Field(...)` (required). The type union `str | None` still allows Gemini to return `null`, but the field MUST be present in the JSON schema's `required` array.\n\n**`FieldMappingValue`** — currently `required: [\"type\"]`, target: `required: [\"type\", \"value\", \"unit\", \"min\", \"max\", \"duration\"]`\n```python\n# BEFORE:\nvalue: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"...\")\n# AFTER:\nvalue: str | None = Field(..., description=\"...\")\n```\n\n**`FieldMappingItem`** — currently `required: [\"entity\", \"relation\", \"value\"]`, target: add `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system`\n\n**`FieldMappingResponse`** — currently `required: []` (empty!), target: `required: [\"mappings\"]`\n\n### Layer 2: Temperature — Reduce Randomness\n\nAdd `temperature=0.1` to the Gemini client in `create_structured_llm()`. Currently NOT set (defaults to model default ~1.0). Lower temperature reduces the probability of entering degenerate repetition states.\n\n### Layer 3: Safety Net — Keep Detection + Retry (already implemented)\n\nKeep the existing defenses from the current uncommitted changes:\n- `is_repetition_loop()` detection in `gemini_utils.py`\n- `check_model_for_repetition()` recursive model inspector\n- `sanitize_repetition_artifacts` Pydantic validator in `FieldMappingValue`\n- 3-attempt retry loop in `generate_field_mappings()`\n\nThese remain as defense-in-depth in case the root cause fix doesn't eliminate 100% of occurrences.\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `services/.../tools/field_mapper.py` | Make all Pydantic fields required (`...` instead of `default=None`) |\n| `services/.../tools/gemini_utils.py` | Add `temperature=0.1` to Gemini client. Keep detection/retry code. |\n\n## What NOT to Change\n\n- Other schemas (`GroundingDecision`, `AgenticReasoningResult`, `OrdinalResolutionResponse`, `LogicDetectionResponse`) — MLFlow shows they don't produce repetition artifacts. Fix those separately if needed later.\n- The `medgemma_decider.py` Gemini calls — they create their own client inline, not through `create_structured_llm()`. No repetition artifacts observed.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Run `uv run python tests/e2e/test_repetition_detection.py` — confirm unit tests still pass\n2. Print the new JSON schema and verify ALL fields appear in `required` arrays\n3. Run the compound criterion reproduction test (Postmenopause, Oral Contraceptive entities) 5x — confirm no repeating digits\n4. `uv run ruff check` on modified files\n5. Commit production changes\n"
}

This is the exact trigger documented by Google.\n\n## Fix: 3 Layers\n\n### Layer 1: Root Cause Fix — Make All Schema Fields Required\n\nChange all `default=None` and `default_factory=list` fields to use `Field(...)` (required). The type union `str | None` still allows Gemini to return `null`, but the field MUST be present in the JSON schema's `required` array.\n\n**`FieldMappingValue`** — currently `required: [\"type\"]`, target: `required: [\"type\", \"value\", \"unit\", \"min\", \"max\", \"duration\"]`\n```python\n# BEFORE:\nvalue: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"...\")\n# AFTER:\nvalue: str | None = Field(..., description=\"...\")\n```\n\n**`FieldMappingItem`** — currently `required: [\"entity\", \"relation\", \"value\"]`, target: add `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system`\n\n**`FieldMappingResponse`** — currently `required: []` (empty!), target: `required: [\"mappings\"]`\n\n### Layer 2: Temperature — Reduce Randomness\n\nAdd `temperature=0.1` to the Gemini client in `create_structured_llm()`. Currently NOT set (defaults to model default ~1.0). Lower temperature reduces the probability of entering degenerate repetition states.\n\n### Layer 3: Safety Net — Keep Detection + Retry (already implemented)\n\nKeep the existing defenses from the current uncommitted changes:\n- `is_repetition_loop()` detection in `gemini_utils.py`\n- `check_model_for_repetition()` recursive model inspector\n- `sanitize_repetition_artifacts` Pydantic validator in `FieldMappingValue`\n- 3-attempt retry loop in `generate_field_mappings()`\n\nThese remain as defense-in-depth in case the root cause fix doesn't eliminate 100% of occurrences.\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `services/.../tools/field_mapper.py` | Make all Pydantic fields required (`...` instead of `default=None`) |\n| `services/.../tools/gemini_utils.py` | Add `temperature=0.1` to Gemini client. Keep detection/retry code. |\n\n## What NOT to Change\n\n- Other schemas (`GroundingDecision`, `AgenticReasoningResult`, `OrdinalResolutionResponse`, `LogicDetectionResponse`) — MLFlow shows they don't produce repetition artifacts. Fix those separately if needed later.\n- The `medgemma_decider.py` Gemini calls — they create their own client inline, not through `create_structured_llm()`. No repetition artifacts observed.\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Run `uv run python tests/e2e/test_repetition_detection.py` — confirm unit tests still pass\n2. Print the new JSON schema and verify ALL fields appear in `required` arrays\n3. Run the compound criterion reproduction test (Postmenopause, Oral Contraceptive entities) 5x — confirm no repeating digits\n4. `uv run ruff check` on modified files\n5. Commit production changes\n"
}

     2→

     4→MedGemma evaluates candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and selects the best
     5→match for each entity. Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical
     6→reasoning, Gemini for structured output parsing.

    33→class AgenticReasoningResult(BaseModel):
    34→    """MedGemma's 3-question reasoning output for failed grounding retry.
    35→
    36→    Used by agentic_reasoning_loop to determine whether to skip an entity,
    37→    apply derived entity mapping, or rephrase the query for a better search.
    38→
    39→    Attributes:
    40→        should_skip: True if entity is not a valid medical criterion.
    41→        is_derived: True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept.
    42→        derived_term: Standard concept term if is_derived is True.
    43→        rephrased_query: Rephrased medical terminology query if applicable.
    44→        gemini_suggestion: Optional additional reformulation from Gemini.
    45→        reasoning: Brief explanation of the reasoning decisions.
    46→    """
    47→
    48→    should_skip: bool = Field(
    49→        default=False,
    50→        description=(
    51→            "True if entity is not a valid medical criterion "
    52→            "(e.g., consent, participation, willingness)"
    53→        ),
    54→    )
    55→    is_derived: bool = Field(
    56→        default=False,
    57→        description="True if entity maps to a more standard medical concept",
    58→    )
    59→    derived_term: str | None = Field(
    60→        default=None,
    61→        description=(
    62→            "Standard concept term if is_derived is True "
    63→            "(e.g., 'age' for 'age >= 18 years')"
    64→        ),
    65→    )
    66→    rephrased_query: str | None = Field(
    67→        default=None,
    68→        description=(
    69→            "Rephrased medical terminology query for better search "
    70→            "(e.g., 'hypertension' for 'high blood pressure')"
    71→        ),
    72→    )
    73→    gemini_suggestion: str | None = Field(
    74→        default=None,
    75→        description=("Optional reformulation suggestion from Gemini structuring step"),
    76→    )
    77→    reasoning: str = Field(
    78→        default="",
    79→        description="Brief explanation of the three-question reasoning",
    80→    )
    81→
    82→
    83→class GroundingDecision(BaseModel):
    84→    """MedGemma's decision for the best terminology match."""
    85→
    86→    selected_code: str | None = Field(
    87→        default=None,
    88→        description=(
    89→            "Selected terminology code (CUI for UMLS, SNOMED code, etc)."
    90→            " Null if no good match."
    91→        ),
    92→    )
    93→    selected_system: str | None = Field(
    94→        default=None,
    95→        description=(
    96→            "The API/system that produced the selected code (e.g. 'umls', 'snomed')."
    97→        ),
    98→    )
    99→    preferred_term: str | None = Field(
   100→        default=None,
   101→        description="Canonical preferred term for the selected code.",
   102→    )
   103→    confidence: float = Field(
   104→        ge=0.0,
   105→        le=1.0,
   106→        description=(
   107→            "Confidence score 0.0-1.0."
   108→            " 0.9-1.0=exact, 0.7-0.8=synonym, 0.5-0.6=partial, 0.0=no match."
   109→        ),
   110→    )
   111→    reasoning: str = Field(
   112→        default="",
   113→        description="Brief explanation for selection.",
   114→    )
   115→
   116→
   117→def _get_medgemma_model() -> Any:
   118→    """Get or create MedGemma model instance."""
   119→    global _model_loader  # noqa: PLW0603
   120→    if _model_loader is None:
   121→        from inference.config import AgentConfig
   122→        from inference.model_garden import create_model_loader
   123→
   124→        _model_loader = create_model_loader(AgentConfig.from_env())
   125→    return _model_loader()
   126→
   127→
   128→async def _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> GroundingDecision:
   129→    """Structure raw MedGemma output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   130→
   131→    Args:
   132→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma output (free-form medical reasoning).
   133→
   134→    Returns:
   135→        GroundingDecision with selected code and confidence.
   136→    """
   137→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   138→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   139→
   140→    if not google_api_key:
   141→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   142→
   143→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   144→
   145→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   146→        model=gemini_model_name,
   147→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   148→        max_output_tokens=2048,
   149→    )
   150→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)
   151→
   152→    prompt = render_template("structure_decision.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text)
   153→
   154→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_decision", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   155→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   156→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   157→        resp_text = str(result) if not isinstance(result, dict) else str(result)
   158→        llm.set_response(resp_text)
   159→
   160→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   161→        return GroundingDecision.model_validate(result)
   162→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   163→
   164→
   165→async def medgemma_decide(
   166→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   167→    candidates: list[GroundingCandidate],
   168→    criterion_context: str,
   169→) -> EntityGroundingResult:
   170→    """Use MedGemma to select the best terminology match for an entity.
   171→
   172→    MedGemma evaluates the candidates returned by TerminologyRouter and
   173→    selects the most appropriate code for the entity. Returns a result with
   174→    confidence=0.0 and no code if no candidates are available or none are
   175→    appropriate.
   176→
   177→    Args:
   178→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   179→        candidates: List of GroundingCandidate objects from TerminologyRouter.
   180→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   181→
   182→    Returns:
   183→        EntityGroundingResult with the selected code, confidence, and reasoning.
   184→    """
   185→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   186→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   187→    criterion_id = entity.get("criterion_id", "")
   188→
   189→    # If no candidates, return result with confidence=0.0
   190→    if not candidates:
   191→        logger.info(
   192→            "No candidates for entity '%s' (type=%s, criterion=%s)"
   193→            " — returning empty grounding",
   194→            entity_text[:50],
   195→            entity_type,
   196→            criterion_id[:12],
   197→        )
   198→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   199→            entity_text=entity_text,
   200→            entity_type=entity_type,
   201→            selected_code=None,
   202→            selected_system=None,
   203→            preferred_term=None,
   204→            confidence=0.0,
   205→            candidates=[],
   206→            reasoning="No terminology candidates available from any API",
   207→        )
   208→
   209→    try:
   210→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   211→
   212→        system_prompt = render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   213→        evaluate_prompt = render_template(
   214→            "grounding_evaluate.jinja2",
   215→            entity_text=entity_text,
   216→            entity_type=entity_type,
   217→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   218→            candidates=candidates,
   219→        )
   220→
   221→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   222→
   223→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   224→        messages = [
   225→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   226→            HumanMessage(content=evaluate_prompt),
   227→        ]
   228→
   229→        with llm_span("medgemma_evaluate", str(model_name)) as llm:
   230→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {evaluate_prompt}")
   231→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   232→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   233→            usage: dict[str, int] = {}
   234→            um = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   235→            if isinstance(um, dict):
   236→                if um.get("input_tokens"):
   237→                    usage["input_tokens"] = um["input_tokens"]
   238→                if um.get("output_tokens"):
   239→                    usage["output_tokens"] = um["output_tokens"]
   240→                if um.get("total_tokens"):
   241→                    usage["total_tokens"] = um["total_tokens"]
   242→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage or None)
   243→
   244→        logger.debug(
   245→            "MedGemma evaluate response for '%s' (first 200 chars): %s",
   246→            entity_text[:30],
   247→            raw_text[:200],
   248→        )
   249→
   250→        decision = await _structure_decision_with_gemini(raw_text)
   251→
   252→        logger.info(
   253→            "Grounding decision for '%s': code=%s, system=%s, conf=%.2f",
   254→            entity_text[:50],
   255→            decision.selected_code,
   256→            decision.selected_system,
   257→            decision.confidence,
   258→        )
   259→
   260→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   261→            entity_text=entity_text,
   262→            entity_type=entity_type,
   263→            selected_code=decision.selected_code,
   264→            selected_system=decision.selected_system,
   265→            preferred_term=decision.preferred_term,
   266→            confidence=decision.confidence,
   267→            candidates=candidates,
   268→            reasoning=decision.reasoning,
   269→        )
   270→
   271→    except Exception as e:
   272→        logger.error(
   273→            "MedGemma decision failed for entity '%s': %s",
   274→            entity_text[:50],
   275→            e,
   276→            exc_info=True,
   277→        )
   278→        return EntityGroundingResult(
   279→            entity_text=entity_text,
   280→            entity_type=entity_type,
   281→            selected_code=None,
   282→            selected_system=None,
   283→            preferred_term=None,
   284→            confidence=0.0,
   285→            candidates=candidates,
   286→            reasoning=f"MedGemma decision failed: {e}",
   287→        )
   288→
   289→
   290→async def _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text: str) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   291→    """Structure raw MedGemma reasoning output using Gemini with_structured_output.
   292→
   293→    Gemini also acts as a collaborating agent here — it can add its own
   294→    reformulation suggestions via the gemini_suggestion field when structuring
   295→    the output.
   296→
   297→    Args:
   298→        raw_text: Raw MedGemma reasoning output (free-form text).
   299→
   300→    Returns:
   301→        AgenticReasoningResult with structured 3-question answers.
   302→    """
   303→    gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   304→    google_api_key = os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY")
   305→
   306→    if not google_api_key:
   307→        raise ValueError("GOOGLE_API_KEY environment variable is required")
   308→
   309→    from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   310→
   311→    gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   312→        model=gemini_model_name,
   313→        google_api_key=google_api_key,
   314→        max_output_tokens=2048,
   315→    )
   316→    structured_llm = gemini.with_structured_output(AgenticReasoningResult)
   317→
   318→    prompt = render_template("structure_reasoning.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text)
   319→
   320→    with llm_span("gemini_structure_reasoning", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   321→        llm.set_request(prompt)
   322→        result = await structured_llm.ainvoke(prompt)
   323→        llm.set_response(str(result))
   324→
   325→    if isinstance(result, dict):
   326→        return AgenticReasoningResult.model_validate(result)
   327→    return result  # type: ignore[return-value]
   328→
   329→
   330→async def agentic_reasoning_loop(
   331→    entity: dict[str, Any],
   332→    criterion_context: str,
   333→    router: "TerminologyRouter",
   334→    attempt: int = 1,
   335→) -> AgenticReasoningResult:
   336→    """Ask MedGemma 3 reasoning questions to determine retry strategy.
   337→
   338→    Called by ground_node when an entity fails initial grounding (zero
   339→    candidates or confidence < 0.5). Asks MedGemma in a single prompt:
   340→    - Q1: Is this a valid medical criterion (or should it be skipped)?
   341→    - Q2: Is this a derived entity that maps to a standard concept?
   342→    - Q3: Can this entity be rephrased for better terminology search?
   343→
   344→    Uses the two-model architecture: MedGemma for medical reasoning, Gemini
   345→    for structured output parsing (and optional reformulation suggestion).
   346→
   347→    Args:
   348→        entity: Entity dict with keys: text, entity_type, criterion_id.
   349→        criterion_context: The full criterion text for context.
   350→        router: TerminologyRouter instance (for get_apis_for_entity context).
   351→        attempt: Current attempt number (1-3) for prompt context.
   352→
   353→    Returns:
   354→        AgenticReasoningResult with should_skip, is_derived, derived_term,
   355→        rephrased_query, gemini_suggestion, and reasoning fields.
   356→        On error, returns default result (no skip, no rephrase) to allow
   357→        the retry loop to continue with the original query.
   358→    """
   359→    entity_text = entity.get("text", "")
   360→    entity_type = entity.get("entity_type", "")
   361→    previous_query = entity.get("_previous_query", entity_text)
   362→
   363→    logger.info(
   364→        "Agentic reasoning (attempt %d) for entity '%s' (type=%s)",
   365→        attempt,
   366→        entity_text[:50],
   367→        entity_type,
   368→    )
   369→
   370→    try:
   371→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   372→
   373→        system_prompt = render_template("grounding_system.jinja2")
   374→        reasoning_prompt = render_template(
   375→            "grounding_reasoning.jinja2",
   376→            entity_text=entity_text,
   377→            entity_type=entity_type,
   378→            criterion_context=criterion_context,
   379→            previous_query=previous_query,
   380→            attempt=attempt,
   381→        )
   382→
   383→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   384→
   385→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   386→        messages = [
   387→            SystemMessage(content=system_prompt),
   388→            HumanMessage(content=reasoning_prompt),
   389→        ]
   390→
   391→        with llm_span("medgemma_reasoning", str(model_name)) as llm:
   392→            llm.set_request(f"[system] {system_prompt}\n\n[user] {reasoning_prompt}")
   393→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   394→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   395→            usage_r: dict[str, int] = {}
   396→            um_r = getattr(raw_response, "usage_metadata", None)
   397→            if isinstance(um_r, dict):
   398→                if um_r.get("input_tokens"):
   399→                    usage_r["input_tokens"] = um_r["input_tokens"]
   400→                if um_r.get("output_tokens"):
   401→                    usage_r["output_tokens"] = um_r["output_tokens"]
   402→                if um_r.get("total_tokens"):
   403→                    usage_r["total_tokens"] = um_r["total_tokens"]
   404→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text), usage_r or None)
   405→
   406→        logger.debug(
   407→            "MedGemma reasoning response for '%s' (first 300 chars): %s",
   408→            entity_text[:30],
   409→            raw_text[:300],
   410→        )
   411→
   412→        result = await _structure_reasoning_with_gemini(raw_text)
   413→
   414→        logger.info(
   415→            "Agentic reasoning result for '%s': skip=%s, derived=%s, "
   416→            "derived_term=%s, rephrased=%s",
   417→            entity_text[:50],
   418→            result.should_skip,
   419→            result.is_derived,
   420→            result.derived_term,
   421→            result.rephrased_query,
   422→        )
   423→
   424→        return result
   425→
   426→    except Exception as e:
   427→        logger.error(
   428→            "Agentic reasoning loop failed for entity '%s': %s",
   429→            entity_text[:50],
   430→            e,
   431→            exc_info=True,
   432→        )
   433→        # Return default result (no skip, no rephrase) — retry with original query
   434→        return AgenticReasoningResult(
   435→            should_skip=False,
   436→            is_derived=False,
   437→            derived_term=None,
   438→            rephrased_query=None,
   439→            reasoning=f"Reasoning loop failed: {e}",
   440→        )
   441→
```

> AGENT

Yes — `medgemma_decider.py` does use MedGemma. Here's the architecture:

1. **`medgemma_decide()`** (line 165): Calls `_get_medgemma_model()` (line 210) which creates a MedGemma model via `inference.model_garden.create_model_loader()`. MedGemma produces free-form medical reasoning text (span: `medgemma_evaluate`).

2. **`_structure_decision_with_gemini()`** (line 128): Takes MedGemma's raw text and passes it to **Gemini** `with_structured_output(GroundingDecision)` to parse it into structured fields (span: `gemini_structure_decision`).

3. **`agentic_reasoning_loop()`** (line 330): Same pattern — MedGemma reasons first (span: `medgemma_reasoning`), then Gemini structures the output via `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()` (span: `gemini_structure_reasoning`).

So the two-model pattern is:
```
MedGemma (medical reasoning, free text) → Gemini (JSON structuring via with_structured_output)
```

Both `GroundingDecision` and `AgenticReasoningResult` also have optional fields with `default=None` — the same pattern that triggers the bug. However, the MLFlow data showed zero repeating-digit artifacts from `gemini_structure_decision` or `gemini_structure_reasoning` spans. Only `gemini_field_mapping` is affected in practice.

This makes sense because:
- `GroundingDecision` has mostly short-value fields (a code, a term, a 0-1 float) — less room for repetition
- `FieldMappingValue` has 5 open-ended string fields where the model can get stuck generating unbounded text

The `medgemma_decider.py` Gemini calls also don't use `create_structured_llm()` — they create their own `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` instances inline (lines 145-149 and 311-315). These also lack `temperature` and penalty parameters.

The type union `str | None` still allows Gemini to return `null`, but the field MUST be present in the JSON schema's `required` array.\n\n**`FieldMappingValue`** — currently `required: [\"type\"]`, target: `required: [\"type\", \"value\", \"unit\", \"min\", \"max\", \"duration\"]`\n```python\n# BEFORE:\nvalue: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"...\")\n# AFTER:\nvalue: str | None = Field(..., description=\"...\")\n```\n\n**`FieldMappingItem`** — currently `required: [\"entity\", \"relation\", \"value\"]`, target: add `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system`\n\n**`FieldMappingResponse`** — currently `required: []` (empty!), target: `required: [\"mappings\"]`\n\n### Layer 2: Generation Parameters — Penalties + Temperature\n\nAdd to the Gemini client in `create_structured_llm()`:\n\n```python\ngemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n    model=gemini_model_name,\n    google_api_key=google_api_key,\n    max_output_tokens=2048,\n    temperature=0.1,                          # was: unset (default ~1.0)\n    model_kwargs={\n        \"frequency_penalty\": 0.8,             # penalize token repetition\n        \"presence_penalty\": 0.5,              # encourage topic diversity\n    },\n)\n```\n\n- **`temperature=0.1`**: Reduces randomness, making degenerate states less likely\n- **`frequency_penalty=0.8`**: Directly penalizes repeated tokens — the primary mechanism to break repetition loops\n- **`presence_penalty=0.5`**: Encourages the model to move to new topics rather than repeating\n\nConfirmed working with `gemini-3-flash-preview` + `with_structured_output()` via LangChain's `model_kwargs`.\n\n### Layer 3: Safety Net — Detection + Retry (already implemented)\n\nKeep the existing defenses from current uncommitted changes:\n- `is_repetition_loop()` detection in `gemini_utils.py`\n- `check_model_for_repetition()` recursive model inspector\n- `sanitize_repetition_artifacts` Pydantic validator in `FieldMappingValue`\n- 3-attempt retry loop in `generate_field_mappings()`\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Change |\n|------|--------|\n| `services/.../tools/field_mapper.py` | Make all Pydantic fields required (`Field(...)` instead of `Field(default=None)`) |\n| `services/.../tools/gemini_utils.py` | Add `temperature=0.1`, `frequency_penalty=0.8`, `presence_penalty=0.5`.    100→    """Extract discrete medical entities from a criterion sentence.
   101→
   102→    Uses Gemini with structured output to decompose a criterion sentence
   103→    into individual medical terms with correct entity types. If the first
   104→    attempt returns zero entities, retries up to *max_retries* times with
   105→    small wording changes before returning an empty list.
   106→
   107→    Args:
   108→        criterion_text: The full criterion sentence to decompose.
   109→        category: Optional category hint from extraction.
   110→        max_retries: Number of retry attempts with rephrased wording (default 2).
   111→
   112→    Returns:
   113→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
   114→    """
   115→    # Normalize unicode operators (≥, ≤, etc.) to ASCII before LLM call
   116→    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)
   117→
   118→    try:
   119→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   120→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
   121→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
   122→            max_output_tokens=2048,
   123→        )
   124→        model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   125→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
   126→
   127→        # Attempt 0: original prompt
   128→        prompt = render_template(
   129→            "entity_decompose.jinja2",
   130→            criterion_text=normalized_text,
   131→            category=category or "",
   132→        )
   133→        decomposed = await _invoke_decompose(
   134→            structured,
   135→            prompt,
   136→            "gemini_entity_decompose",
   137→            model_name,
   138→        )
   139→
   140→        # Retry with rephrased wording if we got 0 entities
   141→        for attempt in range(1, max_retries + 1):
   142→            if decomposed.entities:
   143→                break
   144→            rephrased = _rephrase_for_retry(normalized_text, attempt)
   145→            retry_prompt = render_template(
   146→                "entity_decompose.jinja2",
   147→                criterion_text=rephrased,
   148→                category=category or "",
   149→            )
   150→            logger.info(
   151→                "Entity decomposition retry %d/%d for '%s'",
   152→                attempt,
   153→                max_retries,
   154→                normalized_text[:60],
   155→            )
   156→            decomposed = await _invoke_decompose(
   157→                structured,
   158→                retry_prompt,
   159→                f"gemini_entity_decompose_retry{attempt}",
   160→                model_name,
   161→            )
   162→
   163→        if not decomposed.entities:
   164→            logger.warning(
   165→                "Entity decomposition returned 0 entities after %d retries for '%s'",
   166→                max_retries,
   167→                normalized_text[:80],
   168→            )
   169→
   170→        return [e.model_dump() for e in decomposed.entities]
   171→    except Exception as e:
   172→        logger.error(
   173→            "Entity decomposition failed for criterion '%s': %s",
   174→            normalized_text[:80],
   175→            e,
   176→            exc_info=True,
   177→        )
   178→        return []
   179→
   180→
   181→async def medgemma_decompose_entities(
   182→    criterion_text: str,
   183→) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
   184→    """Fallback: ask MedGemma to identify medical entities when Gemini fails.
   185→
   186→    Uses MedGemma (medical expert) to extract entity names, then Gemini to
   187→    structure the output into typed entities. Called by parse_node when
   188→    Gemini decomposition returns empty even after retries.
   189→
   190→    Args:
   191→        criterion_text: The criterion sentence to decompose.
   192→
   193→    Returns:
   194→        List of dicts with "text" and "entity_type" keys, or empty list on failure.
   195→    """
   196→    normalized_text = _normalize_criterion_text(criterion_text)
   197→
   198→    try:
   199→        from langchain_core.messages import HumanMessage, SystemMessage
   200→
   201→        from protocol_processor.tools.medgemma_decider import _get_medgemma_model
   202→        from protocol_processor.tracing import llm_span
   203→
   204→        model = _get_medgemma_model()
   205→        model_name = getattr(model, "model_name", "") or getattr(model, "model", "")
   206→
   207→        messages = [
   208→            SystemMessage(content=render_template("medgemma_decompose_system.jinja2")),
   209→            HumanMessage(
   210→                content=render_template(
   211→                    "medgemma_decompose_user.jinja2",
   212→                    criterion_text=normalized_text,
   213→                )
   214→            ),
   215→        ]
   216→
   217→        with llm_span("medgemma_entity_decompose", str(model_name)) as llm:
   218→            llm.set_request(str(messages))
   219→            raw_response = await model.ainvoke(messages)
   220→            raw_text = raw_response.content
   221→            llm.set_response(str(raw_text))
   222→
   223→        # Use Gemini to structure MedGemma's free-text into typed entities
   224→        gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
   225→            model=os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash"),
   226→            google_api_key=os.getenv("GOOGLE_API_KEY"),
   227→            max_output_tokens=2048,
   228→        )
   229→        structured = gemini.with_structured_output(DecomposedEntityList)
   230→
   231→        structure_prompt = render_template(
   232→            "structure_entities.jinja2", raw_text=raw_text
   233→        )
   234→        gemini_model_name = os.getenv("GEMINI_MODEL_NAME", "gemini-2.5-flash")
   235→
   236→        with llm_span("gemini_structure_medgemma_decompose", gemini_model_name) as llm:
   237→            llm.set_request(structure_prompt)
   238→            result = await structured.ainvoke(structure_prompt)
   239→            llm.set_response(str(result))

```
     1→# Fix Gemini Structured Output Repetition Loop
     2→
     3→## Context
     4→
     5→Production is producing repeating digit artifacts like `"2202202020202020..."` in field mapping values. Root cause investigation confirmed:
     6→
     7→- **Source**: 100% from `gemini_field_mapping` spans — the `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)` call
     8→- **NOT** MedGemma, structure_builder, or ordinal_resolver
     9→- **Frequency**: 10 out of 547 field_mapping spans (1.8%), concentrated on compound criteria
    10→- **Impact**: Garbage values displayed in the UI
    11→
    12→The documented root cause (per [google-gemini/cookbook#449](https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449) and [discuss.ai.google](https://discuss.ai.google.dev/t/model-deprecations-and-replacements-gemini-flash-2-0/109757)) is that Gemini enters token repetition loops when optional fields lack `required` in the JSON schema.
    13→
    14→**Current `FieldMappingValue` JSON schema has `"required": ["type"]` only** — the 5 string fields (`value`, `unit`, `min`, `max`, `duration`) all use `default=None` and are NOT in the required array. This is the exact trigger documented by Google.
    15→
    16→## Fix: 3 Layers
    17→
    18→### Layer 1: Root Cause — Make All Schema Fields Required
    19→
    20→Change all `default=None` and `default_factory=list` fields to use `Field(...)` (required). The type union `str | None` still allows Gemini to return `null`, but the field MUST be present in the JSON schema's `required` array.
    21→
    22→**`FieldMappingValue`** — currently `required: ["type"]`, target: `required: ["type", "value", "unit", "min", "max", "duration"]`
    23→```python
    24→# BEFORE:
    25→value: str | None = Field(default=None, description="...")
    26→# AFTER:
    27→value: str | None = Field(..., description="...")
    28→```
    29→
    30→**`FieldMappingItem`** — currently `required: ["entity", "relation", "value"]`, target: add `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system`
    31→
    32→**`FieldMappingResponse`** — currently `required: []` (empty!), target: `required: ["mappings"]`
    33→
    34→### Layer 2: Generation Parameters — Penalties + Temperature
    35→
    36→Add to the Gemini client in `create_structured_llm()`:
    37→
    38→```python
    39→gemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(
    40→    model=gemini_model_name,
    41→    google_api_key=google_api_key,
    42→    max_output_tokens=2048,
    43→    temperature=0.1,                          # was: unset (default ~1.0)
    44→    model_kwargs={
    45→        "frequency_penalty": 0.8,             # penalize token repetition
    46→        "presence_penalty": 0.5,              # encourage topic diversity
    47→    },
    48→)
    49→```
    50→
    51→- **`temperature=0.1`**: Reduces randomness, making degenerate states less likely
    52→- **`frequency_penalty=0.8`**: Directly penalizes repeated tokens — the primary mechanism to break repetition loops
    53→- **`presence_penalty=0.5`**: Encourages the model to move to new topics rather than repeating
    54→
    55→Confirmed working with `gemini-3-flash-preview` + `with_structured_output()` via LangChain's `model_kwargs`.
    56→
    57→### Layer 3: Safety Net — Detection + Retry (already implemented)
    58→
    59→Keep the existing defenses from current uncommitted changes:
    60→- `is_repetition_loop()` detection in `gemini_utils.py`
    61→- `check_model_for_repetition()` recursive model inspector
    62→- `sanitize_repetition_artifacts` Pydantic validator in `FieldMappingValue`
    63→- 3-attempt retry loop in `generate_field_mappings()`
    64→
    65→## Files to Modify
    66→
    67→| File | Change |
    68→|------|--------|
    69→| `services/.../tools/field_mapper.py` | Make all Pydantic fields required (`Field(...)` instead of `Field(default=None)`) |
    70→| `services/.../tools/gemini_utils.py` | Add `temperature=0.1`, `frequency_penalty=0.8`, `presence_penalty=0.5`. Keep detection/retry code. |
    71→
    72→## What NOT to Change
    73→
    74→- Other schemas (`GroundingDecision`, `AgenticReasoningResult`, etc.) — MLFlow shows no repetition artifacts. Fix separately if needed.
    75→- `medgemma_decider.py` — uses its own Gemini client, no repetition observed.
    76→
    77→## Verification
    78→
    79→1. Print the new JSON schema — confirm ALL fields appear in `required` arrays
    80→2. Run `uv run python tests/e2e/test_repetition_detection.py` — unit tests pass
    81→3. Run compound criterion reproduction test 5x — confirm no repeating digits
    82→4. `uv run ruff check` on modified files
    83→5. Commit production changes
    84→
```

{
  "file_path": "/Users/noahdolevelixir/.claude-elixirtrials/plans/velvety-whistling-catmull.md",
  "content": "# Fix Gemini Structured Output Repetition Loop\n\n## Context\n\nProduction is producing repeating digit artifacts like `\"2202202020202020...\"` in field mapping values. Root cause investigation confirmed:\n\n- **Source**: 100% from `gemini_field_mapping` spans — `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI.with_structured_output(FieldMappingResponse)`\n- **NOT** MedGemma, structure_builder, or ordinal_resolver\n- **Frequency**: 10 out of 547 field_mapping spans (1.8%), concentrated on compound criteria\n- **Root cause**: Optional Pydantic fields with `default=None` produce JSON schemas without `required`, which triggers the known Gemini repetition loop bug ([cookbook#449](https://github.com/google-gemini/cookbook/issues/449))\n- **DRY problem**: 5 identical `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(...)` instantiations across 3 files — only `create_structured_llm()` would get the fix, leaving 4 inline usages unprotected\n\n## Fix: 3 Layers\n\n### Layer 1: Consolidate All Gemini Structured Output Through `create_structured_llm()`\n\nCurrently 5 places create `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` with identical params:\n\n| File | Function | Schema | Currently uses `create_structured_llm()`? |\n|------|----------|--------|------------------------------------------|\n| `gemini_utils.py:138` | `create_structured_llm()` | any | YES (is the utility) |\n| `medgemma_decider.py:145` | `_structure_decision_with_gemini()` | `GroundingDecision` | NO — inline |\n| `medgemma_decider.py:311` | `_structure_reasoning_with_gemini()` | `AgenticReasoningResult` | NO — inline |\n| `entity_decomposer.py:119` | `decompose_entities()` | `DecomposedEntityList` | NO — inline |\n| `entity_decomposer.py:224` | `medgemma_decompose_entities()` | `DecomposedEntityList` | NO — inline |\n\n**Change**: Replace all 4 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(...)` + `.with_structured_output()` calls with `create_structured_llm(Schema)`. This ensures all Gemini structured output calls get the same parameters, Ollama fallback, and future fixes.\n\nEach callsite handles `None` return (no LLM backend) by raising or returning an empty result, which matches existing error handling patterns.\n\n### Layer 2: Fix Generation Parameters in `create_structured_llm()`\n\nAdd temperature and repetition penalties — applies to ALL 5 callsites via the consolidation:\n\n```python\ngemini = ChatGoogleGenerativeAI(\n    model=gemini_model_name,\n    google_api_key=google_api_key,\n    max_output_tokens=2048,\n    temperature=0.1,                          # was: unset (default ~1.0)\n    model_kwargs={\n        \"frequency_penalty\": 0.8,             # penalize token repetition\n        \"presence_penalty\": 0.5,              # encourage topic diversity\n    },\n)\n```\n\n### Layer 3: Make All Pydantic Schema Fields Required\n\nChange `default=None` / `default_factory=list` to `Field(...)` so ALL fields appear in JSON schema `required` arrays:\n\n**`FieldMappingValue`** — `required: [\"type\"]` -> `required: [\"type\", \"value\", \"unit\", \"min\", \"max\", \"duration\"]`\n**`FieldMappingItem`** — add `unit`, `value_concept_id`, `value_concept_system` to required\n**`FieldMappingResponse`** — `required: []` -> `required: [\"mappings\"]`\n\n```python\n# BEFORE:\nvalue: str | None = Field(default=None, description=\"...\")\n# AFTER:\nvalue: str | None = Field(..., description=\"...\")\n```\n\n### Layer 4: Safety Net — Detection + Retry (already implemented)\n\nKeep the existing defenses in `gemini_utils.py` and `field_mapper.py`:\n- `is_repetition_loop()` detection\n- `check_model_for_repetition()` recursive inspector\n- `sanitize_repetition_artifacts` Pydantic validator\n- 3-attempt retry loop in `generate_field_mappings()`\n\n## Files to Modify\n\n| File | Changes |\n|------|---------|\n| `services/.../tools/gemini_utils.py` | Add `temperature=0.1`, `frequency_penalty`, `presence_penalty` to `create_structured_llm()`. Keep detection/retry code. |\n| `services/.../tools/field_mapper.py` | Make all Pydantic fields required (`Field(...)` instead of `Field(default=None)`) |\n| `services/.../tools/medgemma_decider.py` | Replace 2 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` blocks with `create_structured_llm()`. Remove `from langchain_google_genai import ChatGoogleGenerativeAI`. |\n| `services/.../tools/entity_decomposer.py` | Replace 2 inline `ChatGoogleGenerativeAI` blocks with `create_structured_llm()`. Remove inline import. |\n\n## Verification\n\n1. Print new JSON schemas — confirm ALL fields in `required` arrays\n2. `uv run python tests/e2e/test_repetition_detection.py` — unit tests pass\n3. Run compound criterion reproduction test 5x — confirm no repeating digits\n4. `uv run ruff check` on all 4 modified files\n5. Commit production changes\n"
}
```
